 ## 八、配置参考
 
+### 8.0 process-config 配置键：id-pattern
+
+在 `apriori/process-config.md` 写一行 `| id-pattern | <裸 JS 正则源串> |`，一处声明项目的场景 ID 形状，处处生效。解析优先级：`--id-pattern` flag（仅 verify 与 gate；按存在性判定——空 flag 是错误，绝不回退）> 配置行 > 内置默认 `[A-Z]+-\d+`。`check`（CK-04）与 `doctor`（D6）只吃配置行、无 flag。四个消费点用同一识别契约：从标题第一个字符开始匹配，后继为字母/数字/下划线则拒绝，不额外拼接 `\b`，源串按原样编译。
+
+pipe 转义分两层，切勿混为一谈：表格单元格内属于值的每个 pipe 都写 `\|`（如 alternation 单元格 `(AC\|BR)-\d+` 解析为正则源串 `(AC|BR)-\d+`，裸 `|` 即 alternation）；正则要**匹配**字面 pipe 字符时用字符类——单元格里写 `[\|]`，解析为 `[|]`。该转义规则对全部配置键统一生效。
+
+错误在消费时上浮且 fail-closed，消息指明来源（`--id-pattern` 或 `process-config`）：verify 与 gate 按既有文本/JSON 错误形状 exit 2；check 打印 `RESULT: ERROR`（exit 2）；doctor 报 D6 finding 并跳过 D5 探针（结果 FINDINGS，exit 1）——绝不静默回退默认。配置来源的 pattern 是 CI 自动消费的仓库输入，其匹配在可终止子进程内执行（超预算即杀——灾难性回溯的配置行无法挂死 CI）；flag 是操作者交互输入，进程内执行。
+

 const fs = require('fs');
 const path = require('path');
 
+// The built-in scenario-ID pattern. Lives here (zero-dep module) so every consumer —
+// spec-runner, gate, check, doctor — resolves through one place; spec-runner re-exports it.
+const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
+
+// Cell splitting honors the markdown pipe escape by backslash parity (CF-08/CF-09):
+// an odd run of backslashes before a pipe escapes it (the escaping backslash is removed,
+// the pipe joins the value); an even run keeps the pipe a separator, backslashes literal.
+// No other backslash sequence is ever unescaped — this is not a markdown renderer.
+function splitCells(line) {
+  const cells = [];
+  let cur = '', run = 0;
+  for (const ch of line) {
+    if (ch === '\\') { run++; cur += ch; continue; }
+    if (ch === '|') {
+      if (run % 2 === 1) { cur = cur.slice(0, -1) + '|'; }   // drop the escaping backslash, keep the pipe
+      else { cells.push(cur); cur = ''; }
+      run = 0;
+      continue;
+    }
+    run = 0;
+    cur += ch;
+  }
+  cells.push(cur);
+  return cells;
+}
+

+// Message sanitization is the single exit for pattern errors: control chars stripped,
+// total length capped INCLUDING the ellipsis; the engine's e.message is never concatenated.
+function sanitizeMsg(msg) {
+  const clean = msg.replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '·');
+  return clean.length > 200 ? clean.slice(0, 199) + '…' : clean;
+}
+function boundedSource(s) {
+  const clean = String(s).replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '·');
+  return clean.length > 80 ? clean.slice(0, 79) + '…' : clean;
+}
+
+// Effective id-pattern: flag (by PRESENCE — an empty flag is a flag-origin error, never a
+// config fallback) > config `id-pattern` row > DEFAULT_ID.
+// → { source, origin: 'flag'|'config'|'default' } | { error }
+function resolveIdPattern(cwd, flagValue) {
+  if (flagValue !== null && flagValue !== undefined) {
+    if (flagValue === '') return { error: 'empty --id-pattern' };
+    try { new RegExp(flagValue); } catch { return { error: sanitizeMsg(`invalid --id-pattern '${boundedSource(flagValue)}' (regex does not compile)`) }; }
+    return { source: flagValue, origin: 'flag' };
+  }
+  const { value, problem } = getConfig(cwd, 'id-pattern');
+  if (problem) return { error: problem };
+  if (value !== null) {
+    try { new RegExp(value); } catch { return { error: sanitizeMsg(`process-config id-pattern row is invalid: '${boundedSource(value)}' (regex does not compile)`) }; }
+    return { source: value, origin: 'config' };
+  }
+  return { source: DEFAULT_ID, origin: 'default' };
+}
+
+module.exports = { parseConfig, readConfig, getConfig, splitCells, DEFAULT_ID, resolveIdPattern, sanitizeMsg, boundedSource };

 function stripFences(text) { return text.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ''); }
 
+// ---- the id matcher: one recognition contract, two execution channels -------------------
+// A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically; its every
+// actual application runs inside a terminable child (SIGKILL on budget) so a catastrophic
+// pattern can never hang the CLI. Flag/default origins are operator/built-in trust: inline.
+const CHILD_PATH = path.join(__dirname, 'id-match-child.js');
+let childRunnerOverride = null;                 // test seam — SR-55 injects failures here
+function _setChildRunner(fn) { childRunnerOverride = fn; }
+function defaultChildRunner(payload) {
+  const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [CHILD_PATH], {
+    input: payload, timeout: 2000, killSignal: 'SIGKILL', shell: false,
+    encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 8 * 1024 * 1024,
+  });
+  return { status: r.status, signal: r.signal || null, stdout: r.stdout || '', error: r.error || null };
+}
+// resolved = resolveIdPattern's success shape → { batch(texts) → {ids:(string|null)[]} | {failure} }
+function makeIdMatcher(resolved) {
+  if (resolved.origin !== 'config') {
+    const re = new RegExp(resolved.source);
+    return { batch: (texts) => ({ ids: texts.map((t) => leadId(t, re)) }) };
+  }
+  return { batch: (texts) => {
+    const runner = childRunnerOverride || defaultChildRunner;
+    let r;
+    try { r = runner(JSON.stringify({ pattern: resolved.source, texts })); }
+    catch (e) { return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(e.message || e)}` }; }
+    if (r.error) return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(r.error.message || r.error)}` };
+    if (r.signal) return { failure: r.signal === 'SIGKILL' ? 'timeout (budget exceeded, child killed)' : `signal ${r.signal}` };
+    if (r.status !== 0) return { failure: `non-zero-exit (${r.status})` };
+    let parsed;
+    try { parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { return { failure: 'malformed-output (not JSON)' }; }
+    if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.ids) || parsed.ids.length !== texts.length ||
+        parsed.ids.some((x) => x !== null && typeof x !== 'string'))
+      return { failure: 'malformed-output (bad shape)' };
+    return { ids: parsed.ids };
+  } };
+}

+<!-- id-pattern pipe escaping (two separate layers — do not conflate them):
+     inside a table cell, EVERY pipe belonging to the value is written \| — so an
+     alternation like (AC\|BR)-\d+ parses to the regex source (AC|BR)-\d+ (a bare | = alternation);
+     to MATCH a literal pipe character, use a character class: write [\|] in the cell,
+     which parses to the regex source [|]. Precedence: --id-pattern flag (verify/gate) >
+     this row > built-in default [A-Z]+-\d+. An uncompilable row is a consumption-time
+     error (verify/gate/check exit 2; doctor reports a D6 finding) — never a silent fallback. -->

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * process-config parses as structure, never as full-text regex (config-contract).
     4	 * One shared reader for every consumer: fenced code blocks and HTML comments are
     5	 * non-content (unterminated blocks make the rest of the file inert — naturally
     6	 * fail-closed for waiver keys); a config row is any |-leading table row, first
     7	 * cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored; same-key same-value
     8	 * duplicates are tolerated, different values are a CONFLICT problem. Problems
     9	 * surface only when the key is actually consumed.
    10	 */
    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	
    14	// The built-in scenario-ID pattern. Lives here (zero-dep module) so every consumer —
    15	// spec-runner, gate, check, doctor — resolves through one place; spec-runner re-exports it.
    16	const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
    17	
    18	// Cell splitting honors the markdown pipe escape by backslash parity (CF-08/CF-09):
    19	// an odd run of backslashes before a pipe escapes it (the escaping backslash is removed,
    20	// the pipe joins the value); an even run keeps the pipe a separator, backslashes literal.
    21	// No other backslash sequence is ever unescaped — this is not a markdown renderer.
    22	function splitCells(line) {
    23	  const cells = [];
    24	  let cur = '', run = 0;
    25	  for (const ch of line) {
    26	    if (ch === '\\') { run++; cur += ch; continue; }
    27	    if (ch === '|') {
    28	      if (run % 2 === 1) { cur = cur.slice(0, -1) + '|'; }   // drop the escaping backslash, keep the pipe
    29	      else { cells.push(cur); cur = ''; }
    30	      run = 0;
    31	      continue;
    32	    }
    33	    run = 0;
    34	    cur += ch;
    35	  }
    36	  cells.push(cur);
    37	  return cells;
    38	}
    39	
    40	// parse the raw text → { values: Map<key,value>, conflicts: Set<key> }
    41	function parseConfig(text) {
    42	  const values = new Map();
    43	  const conflicts = new Set();
    44	  let fence = false, comment = false;
    45	  for (const raw of (text || '').split('\n')) {
    46	    const line = raw.endsWith('\r') ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;
    47	    let t = line;
    48	    if (comment) {
    49	      const close = t.indexOf('-->');
    50	      if (close < 0) continue;
    51	      comment = false;
    52	      t = t.slice(close + 3);
    53	    }
    54	    if (fence) { if (/^\s*```/.test(t)) fence = false; continue; }
    55	    if (/^\s*```/.test(t)) { fence = true; continue; }
    56	    // strip inline comment SPANS — content before the opener stays live
    57	    while (t.includes('<!--')) {
    58	      const open = t.indexOf('<!--');
    59	      const close = t.indexOf('-->', open + 4);
    60	      if (close < 0) { t = t.slice(0, open); comment = true; break; }
    61	      t = t.slice(0, open) + t.slice(close + 3);
    62	    }
    63	    if (!t.trimStart().startsWith('|')) continue;
    64	    const cells = splitCells(t).map((c) => c.trim());
    65	    // cells[0] is the empty prefix before the leading pipe
    66	    const key = cells[1], value = cells[2];
    67	    if (!key || value === undefined || value === '') continue;
    68	    if (key.toLowerCase() === 'key' || /^-+$/.test(key)) continue;      // header / separator
    69	    if (/^-+$/.test(value)) continue;
    70	    if (values.has(key) && values.get(key) !== value) { conflicts.add(key); continue; }
    71	    values.set(key, value);
    72	  }
    73	  return { values, conflicts };
    74	}
    75	
    76	function readConfig(cwd) {
    77	  const p = path.join(cwd || '.', 'apriori', 'process-config.md');
    78	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) return { values: new Map(), conflicts: new Set() };
    79	  let text;
    80	  try { text = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'); }
    81	  catch (e) {   // present but unreadable (directory, permissions, …) — a consumption-time problem, never a throw
    82	    return { values: new Map(), conflicts: new Set(), unreadable: String(e.code || e.message) };
    83	  }
    84	  return parseConfig(text);
    85	}
    86	
    87	// one key → { value: string|null, problem: string|null } (problem only when consumed)
    88	function getConfig(cwd, key) {
    89	  const { values, conflicts, unreadable } = readConfig(cwd);
    90	  if (unreadable) return { value: null, problem: `apriori/process-config.md exists but cannot be read (${unreadable}) — fix the file` };
    91	  if (conflicts.has(key)) return { value: null, problem: `process-config carries conflicting '${key}' rows — resolve the conflict (one live row per key)` };
    92	  return { value: values.has(key) ? values.get(key) : null, problem: null };
    93	}
    94	
    95	// Message sanitization is the single exit for pattern errors: control chars stripped,
    96	// total length capped INCLUDING the ellipsis; the engine's e.message is never concatenated.
    97	function sanitizeMsg(msg) {
    98	  const clean = msg.replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '·');
    99	  return clean.length > 200 ? clean.slice(0, 199) + '…' : clean;
   100	}
   101	function boundedSource(s) {
   102	  const clean = String(s).replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '·');
   103	  return clean.length > 80 ? clean.slice(0, 79) + '…' : clean;
   104	}
   105	
   106	// Effective id-pattern: flag (by PRESENCE — an empty flag is a flag-origin error, never a
   107	// config fallback) > config `id-pattern` row > DEFAULT_ID.
   108	// → { source, origin: 'flag'|'config'|'default' } | { error }
   109	function resolveIdPattern(cwd, flagValue) {
   110	  if (flagValue !== null && flagValue !== undefined) {
   111	    if (flagValue === '') return { error: 'empty --id-pattern' };
   112	    try { new RegExp(flagValue); } catch { return { error: sanitizeMsg(`invalid --id-pattern '${boundedSource(flagValue)}' (regex does not compile)`) }; }
   113	    return { source: flagValue, origin: 'flag' };
   114	  }
   115	  const { value, problem } = getConfig(cwd, 'id-pattern');
   116	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   117	  if (value !== null) {
   118	    try { new RegExp(value); } catch { return { error: sanitizeMsg(`process-config id-pattern row is invalid: '${boundedSource(value)}' (regex does not compile)`) }; }
   119	    return { source: value, origin: 'config' };
   120	  }
   121	  return { source: DEFAULT_ID, origin: 'default' };
   122	}
   123	
   124	module.exports = { parseConfig, readConfig, getConfig, splitCells, DEFAULT_ID, resolveIdPattern, sanitizeMsg, boundedSource };
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * id-match-child — the terminable matcher for CONFIG-sourced id-patterns.
     4	 * A config row is repository input that CI consumes automatically; applying it in-process
     5	 * would let a catastrophic-backtracking pattern hang the CLI. This fixed script (never
     6	 * source-interpolated) is spawned shell:false with {pattern, texts} as stdin data and
     7	 * answers {ids} on stdout; the parent SIGKILLs it on budget. Exit codes: 0 ok · 1 bad input.
     8	 */
     9	const { leadId } = require('./spec-runner');
    10	
    11	let raw = '';
    12	process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
    13	process.stdin.on('data', (c) => { raw += c; });
    14	process.stdin.on('end', () => {
    15	  let req;
    16	  try { req = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { process.exit(1); }
    17	  if (!req || typeof req.pattern !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(req.texts)) process.exit(1);
    18	  let re;
    19	  try { re = new RegExp(req.pattern); } catch { process.exit(1); }
    20	  const ids = req.texts.map((t) => (typeof t === 'string' ? leadId(t, re) : null));
    21	  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ ids }));
    22	});
     1	'use strict';
     2	// config-contract — CF-01..07: process-config parses as structure
     3	const { test } = require('node:test');
     4	const assert = require('node:assert');
     5	const fs = require('fs');
     6	const os = require('os');
     7	const path = require('path');
     8	const { spawnSync } = require('node:child_process');
     9	
    10	const BIN = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'bin', 'apriori.js');
    11	function run(args, cwd) { return spawnSync('node', [BIN, ...args], { encoding: 'utf8', cwd }); }
    12	
    13	const STORE = '### Requirement: Alpha\nold\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 a\n- t\n';
    14	const MOD = '## MODIFIED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Alpha\nCHANGED\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 a\n- t\n';
    15	function proj(config) {
    16	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-cfg-'));
    17	  for (const [rel, c] of Object.entries({
    18	    'apriori/specs/a/spec.md': STORE,
    19	    'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md': 'change: c\ntier: medium\n',
    20	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': MOD,
    21	    'apriori/process-config.md': config,
    22	  })) { fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(path.join(root, rel)), { recursive: true }); fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, rel), c); }
    23	  return root;
    24	}
    25	const store = (root) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/specs/a/spec.md'), 'utf8');

    27	test('CF-01 fenced and commented rows never take effect', () => {
    28	  const denied = proj('```md\n| cas | optional |\n```\n\n| cas | required |\n');
    29	  const r1 = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write'], denied);
    30	  assert.strictEqual(r1.status, 1, r1.stdout + r1.stderr);
    31	  assert.doesNotMatch(store(denied), /CHANGED/);
    32	  const waived = proj('```md\n| cas | required |\n```\n\n| cas | optional |\n');
    33	  const r2 = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write'], waived);
    34	  assert.strictEqual(r2.status, 0, r2.stdout + r2.stderr);
    35	  assert.match(r2.stdout + r2.stderr, /waived|process-config/i);
    36	  assert.match(store(waived), /CHANGED/);
    37	  const comment = proj('<!--\n| cas | optional |\n-->\n');
    38	  const r3 = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write'], comment);
    39	  assert.strictEqual(r3.status, 1, r3.stdout + r3.stderr);
    40	});

    45	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr);
    46	  assert.match(store(root), /CHANGED/);
    47	  // an inline HTML comment is a span, not a line-killer — the live row survives (CCIMPL-2)
    48	  const inline = proj('| cas | optional | <!-- note --> |\n');

   115	  assert.strictEqual(four.values.get('k'), 'a\\\\\\\\');

     2	// gate-id-pattern — SR-50..SR-55, GT-22..GT-25, CK-13..CK-16, DR-16..DR-18:
     3	// the effective id-pattern resolves flag > config > default, one contract for four consumers.
     4	const { test } = require('node:test');
     5	const assert = require('node:assert');
     6	const fs = require('fs');
     7	const os = require('os');
     8	const path = require('path');
     9	const { spawnSync } = require('node:child_process');
    10	
    11	const BIN = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'bin', 'apriori.js');
    12	const PATTERN = '[A-Z]+(-[A-Z]+)*-\\d+[a-z]*';
    13	const SPEC3 = '#### Scenario: AC-01 plain\n#### Scenario: AC-08a suffixed\n#### Scenario: AC-BIS-01 multi\n';
    14	const TAP3 = `node -e "['AC-01 plain','AC-08a suffixed','AC-BIS-01 multi'].forEach((t,i)=>console.log('ok '+(i+1)+' - '+t))"`;
    15	const CTRL = new RegExp('[\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]');
    16	
    17	function proj(files) {
    18	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-idp-'));
    19	  for (const [rel, c] of Object.entries(files)) {
    20	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(path.join(root, rel)), { recursive: true });
    21	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, rel), c);
    22	  }
    23	  return root;
    24	}
    25	const run = (root, args) => spawnSync('node', [BIN, ...args], { encoding: 'utf8', cwd: root });
    26	const vrun = (root, args) => run(root, ['verify', ...args]);
    27	const marker = (root) => path.join(root, 'ran.marker');
    28	const markerCmd = (root) => `node -e "require('fs').writeFileSync(${JSON.stringify(marker(root)).replace(/"/g, '\\"')},'x');console.log('1..0')"`;

    32	test('SR-50 the config row takes effect without a flag', () => {
    33	  const files = { 'apriori/specs/m/spec.md': SPEC3 };
    34	  const bare = proj(files);
    35	  const r1 = JSON.parse(vrun(bare, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--test-cmd', TAP3, '--json']).stdout);
    36	  assert.strictEqual(r1.unidentified.length, 2, 'without the row only AC-01 is identified');
    37	  const cfg = proj({ ...files, 'apriori/process-config.md': `| id-pattern | ${PATTERN} |\n` });
    38	  const out = vrun(cfg, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--test-cmd', TAP3, '--json']);
    39	  const r2 = JSON.parse(out.stdout);
    40	  assert.strictEqual(r2.unidentified.length, 0, 'config row identifies all three: ' + out.stdout + out.stderr);
    41	  assert.strictEqual(r2.result, 'GREEN');
    42	});

    44	test('SR-51 the flag overrides and shields the config', () => {

    48	  });

    50	  const j = JSON.parse(r.stdout);
    51	  assert.strictEqual(j.result, 'GREEN', r.stdout + r.stderr);   // flag binds; broken config never consulted
    52	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 0);
    53	});

    55	test('SR-52 an invalid effective pattern refuses to run', () => {
    56	  // flag origin
    57	  const a = proj({ 'apriori/specs/m/spec.md': SPEC3 });
    58	  const ra = vrun(a, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--id-pattern', '(', '--test-cmd', markerCmd(a), '--json']);
    59	  assert.strictEqual(ra.status, 2, ra.stdout + ra.stderr);
    60	  const ja = JSON.parse(ra.stdout);
    61	  assert.strictEqual(ja.result, 'ERROR');
    62	  assert.match(ja.errors.join(' '), /--id-pattern/);
    63	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(marker(a)), 'test command never spawned (flag origin)');
    64	  // empty flag never falls back to config
    65	  const e = proj({ 'apriori/specs/m/spec.md': SPEC3, 'apriori/process-config.md': `| id-pattern | ${PATTERN} |\n` });
    66	  const re = vrun(e, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--id-pattern', '', '--test-cmd', markerCmd(e), '--json']);
    67	  assert.strictEqual(re.status, 2);
    68	  assert.match(JSON.parse(re.stdout).errors.join(' '), /empty --id-pattern/);
    69	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(marker(e)));
    70	  // config origin
    71	  const c = proj({ 'apriori/specs/m/spec.md': SPEC3, 'apriori/process-config.md': '| id-pattern | ( |\n' });
    72	  const rc = vrun(c, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--test-cmd', markerCmd(c), '--json']);
    73	  assert.strictEqual(rc.status, 2);
    74	  assert.match(JSON.parse(rc.stdout).errors.join(' '), /process-config/);
    75	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(marker(c)), 'test command never spawned (config origin)');
    76	  // whole-message sanitization: control chars stripped, bounded incl. ellipsis, no engine re-leak
    77	  const evil = '(' + 'x'.repeat(300);
    78	  const rs = vrun(a, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--id-pattern', evil, '--test-cmd', markerCmd(a), '--json']);
    79	  const errs = JSON.parse(rs.stdout).errors;
    80	  for (const piece of errs) {
    81	    assert.doesNotMatch(piece, CTRL, 'no control characters');
    82	    assert.ok(piece.length <= 200, `bounded incl ellipsis: ${piece.length}`);
    83	  }
    84	  // --change --json keeps the projection field on the error path
    85	  const ch = proj({
    86	    'apriori/specs/m/spec.md': '### Requirement: R1\n\n#### Scenario: AC-01 plain\n- t\n',
    87	    'apriori/changes/z/flow-state.md': 'change: z\ntier: medium\n',
    88	    'apriori/changes/z/specs/m/spec.md': '## ADDED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: R2\n\n#### Scenario: AC-02 b\n- t\n',
    89	    'apriori/process-config.md': '| id-pattern | ( |\n',
    90	  });
    91	  const rch = vrun(ch, ['--change', 'z', '--test-cmd', markerCmd(ch), '--json']);
    92	  assert.strictEqual(rch.status, 2, rch.stdout + rch.stderr);
    93	  const jch = JSON.parse(rch.stdout);
    94	  assert.ok(jch.projection, 'projection survives the early exit');
    95	  assert.deepStrictEqual(jch.projection.modules, ['m/spec.md']);
    96	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(marker(ch)));
    97	});

   105	test('SR-54 catastrophic config matching is terminated, adversarial titles included', () => {
   106	  const evilTitle = 'a'.repeat(40) + '!';
   107	  const root = proj({
   108	    'apriori/specs/m/spec.md': `#### Scenario: ${evilTitle}\n`,

   111	  const t0 = Date.now();
   112	  const r = vrun(root, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--test-cmd', markerCmd(root), '--json']);
   113	  assert.ok(Date.now() - t0 < 15000, 'terminated within budget');
   114	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 2, r.stdout + r.stderr);
   115	  assert.match(JSON.parse(r.stdout).errors.join(' '), /process-config/);
   116	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(marker(root)), 'test command never spawned');
   117	  // the real multi-segment pattern completes normally through the child
   118	  const ok = proj({ 'apriori/specs/m/spec.md': SPEC3, 'apriori/process-config.md': `| id-pattern | ${PATTERN} |\n` });
   119	  const rok = vrun(ok, ['--specs', 'apriori/specs', '--test-cmd', TAP3, '--json']);
   120	  assert.strictEqual(JSON.parse(rok.stdout).result, 'GREEN');

   123	test('SR-55 every child failure class fails closed', () => {
   124	  const sr = require('../lib/spec-runner');
   125	  const resolvedCfg = { source: '[A-Z]+-\\d+', origin: 'config' };
   126	  const classes = [
   127	    ['timeout', { status: null, signal: 'SIGKILL', stdout: '', error: null }],
   128	    ['spawn-error', { status: null, signal: null, stdout: '', error: new Error('ENOENT') }],
   129	    ['signal', { status: null, signal: 'SIGSEGV', stdout: '{"ids":[]}', error: null }],
   130	    ['non-zero-exit', { status: 1, signal: null, stdout: '', error: null }],
   131	    ['malformed-not-json', { status: 0, signal: null, stdout: 'nope', error: null }],
   132	    ['malformed-length', { status: 0, signal: null, stdout: '{"ids":[]}', error: null }],       // batch of 1 vs length 0
   133	    ['malformed-element', { status: 0, signal: null, stdout: '{"ids":[42]}', error: null }],
   134	  ];
   135	  for (const [label, fake] of classes) {
   136	    sr._setChildRunner(() => fake);
   137	    const m = sr.makeIdMatcher(resolvedCfg);
   138	    const out = m.batch(['AC-01 x']);
   139	    assert.ok(out.failure, `${label} fails closed`);
   140	  }
   141	  // well-formed response binds
   142	  sr._setChildRunner(() => ({ status: 0, signal: null, stdout: '{"ids":["AC-01"]}', error: null }));
   143	  assert.deepStrictEqual(sr.makeIdMatcher(resolvedCfg).batch(['AC-01 x']), { ids: ['AC-01'] });
   144	  // flag/default origins never touch the child
   145	  sr._setChildRunner(() => { throw new Error('child must not be called'); });
   146	  for (const origin of ['flag', 'default']) {
   147	    const m = sr.makeIdMatcher({ source: '[A-Z]+-\\d+', origin });
   148	    assert.deepStrictEqual(m.batch(['AC-01 x']), { ids: ['AC-01'] });
   149	  }
   150	  sr._setChildRunner(null);   // restore default
   151	});

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * spec-runner — bind spec scenario IDs to test runs, report per-scenario red/green.
     4	 * VISION endgame, weak form: "spec IS the test suite". Zero deps — pure Node stdlib.
     5	 * Coupling surface is tiny and language-agnostic: markdown scenarios + a TAP-emitting test command.
     6	 *
     7	 * FAIL-CLOSED: a deterministic gate must refuse to say GREEN when its inputs are missing,
     8	 * its instrument misbehaved, or its evidence is ambiguous. Vacuous success is a bug.
     9	 * Exit codes: 0 = GREEN · 1 = spec/test gaps · 2 = invocation or infrastructure error.
    10	 */
    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    14	// merge-family helpers shared with archive (3.1 projection); archive-merge never requires spec-runner
    15	const am = require('./archive-merge');
    16	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    17	// DEFAULT_ID lives in config (the zero-dep resolution home) and is re-exported here unchanged
    18	const { DEFAULT_ID, resolveIdPattern, sanitizeMsg } = require('./config');
    19	const SCENARIO_RE = /^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/gm;
    20	// TAP result line, directive split off separately (# SKIP / # TODO)
    21	const TAP_RE = /^(ok|not ok)\s+\d+\s+-\s+(.*)$/gm;
    22	
    23	// Leading scenario/test ID. The match must end at a word boundary: `XX-01b` is NOT `XX-01` —
    24	// silently truncating would merge two different scenarios into one binding.
    25	function leadId(text, idRe) {
    26	  const m = text.trim().match(idRe);
    27	  if (!m || m.index !== 0) return null;
    28	  const next = text.trim()[m[0].length];
    29	  if (next !== undefined && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(next)) return null;
    30	  return m[0];
    31	}
    32	
    33	function stripFences(text) { return text.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ''); }
    34	
    35	// ---- the id matcher: one recognition contract, two execution channels -------------------
    36	// A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically; its every
    37	// actual application runs inside a terminable child (SIGKILL on budget) so a catastrophic
    38	// pattern can never hang the CLI. Flag/default origins are operator/built-in trust: inline.
    39	const CHILD_PATH = path.join(__dirname, 'id-match-child.js');
    40	let childRunnerOverride = null;                 // test seam — SR-55 injects failures here
    41	function _setChildRunner(fn) { childRunnerOverride = fn; }
    42	function defaultChildRunner(payload) {
    43	  const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [CHILD_PATH], {
    44	    input: payload, timeout: 2000, killSignal: 'SIGKILL', shell: false,
    45	    encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 8 * 1024 * 1024,
    46	  });
    47	  return { status: r.status, signal: r.signal || null, stdout: r.stdout || '', error: r.error || null };
    48	}
    49	// resolved = resolveIdPattern's success shape → { batch(texts) → {ids:(string|null)[]} | {failure} }
    50	function makeIdMatcher(resolved) {
    51	  if (resolved.origin !== 'config') {
    52	    const re = new RegExp(resolved.source);
    53	    return { batch: (texts) => ({ ids: texts.map((t) => leadId(t, re)) }) };
    54	  }
    55	  return { batch: (texts) => {
    56	    const runner = childRunnerOverride || defaultChildRunner;
    57	    let r;
    58	    try { r = runner(JSON.stringify({ pattern: resolved.source, texts })); }
    59	    catch (e) { return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(e.message || e)}` }; }
    60	    if (r.error) return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(r.error.message || r.error)}` };
    61	    if (r.signal) return { failure: r.signal === 'SIGKILL' ? 'timeout (budget exceeded, child killed)' : `signal ${r.signal}` };
    62	    if (r.status !== 0) return { failure: `non-zero-exit (${r.status})` };
    63	    let parsed;
    64	    try { parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { return { failure: 'malformed-output (not JSON)' }; }
    65	    if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.ids) || parsed.ids.length !== texts.length ||
    66	        parsed.ids.some((x) => x !== null && typeof x !== 'string'))
    67	      return { failure: 'malformed-output (bad shape)' };
    68	    return { ids: parsed.ids };
    69	  } };
    70	}
    71	// the one sanitized failure text every consumer surfaces for a config-origin matcher failure
    72	function matcherFailureMsg(failure) {
    73	  return sanitizeMsg(`process-config id-pattern matching failed (${failure})`);
    74	}
    75	const inlineMatcher = (idRe) => makeIdMatcher({ source: idRe.source, origin: 'default' });
    76	
    77	function mdFiles(target) {
    78	  if (!fs.existsSync(target)) return [];
    79	  const st = fs.statSync(target);
    80	  if (st.isFile()) return [target];
    81	  const out = [];
    82	  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(target, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    83	    const p = path.join(target, e.name);
    84	    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...mdFiles(p));
    85	    else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
    86	  }
    87	  return out.sort();
    88	}
    89	
    90	// Shared scenario scan over one text — STRUCTURE ONLY (no ID extraction; that is the
    91	// matcher's job so config-origin patterns stay inside the terminable channel).
    92	// Fenced content is documentation; scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks
    93	// (the marker REMOVED injects) are excluded in every form — they are neither demanded
    94	// nor listed, and their lingering tests become ORPHAN.
    95	function scanTitles(text, label, pairs) {
    96	  const clean = am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
    97	  let m;
    98	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    99	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) pairs.push([label, m[1]]);
   100	}
   101	
   102	// Batch-bind (label,title) pairs through a matcher → acc, or {failure} when the matcher fails.
   103	function bindPairs(pairs, matcher) {
   104	  const res = matcher.batch(pairs.map(([, title]) => title));
   105	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   106	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   107	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   108	    const id = res.ids[i];
   109	    if (id) {
   110	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
   111	      acc.byId.get(id).push(title);
   112	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
   113	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, title]);
   114	  });
   115	  return acc;
   116	}
   117	
   118	function dupsOf(acc) {
   119	  return [...acc.byId.entries()].filter(([, titles]) => titles.length > 1)
   120	    .map(([id]) => ({ id, files: [...new Set(acc.idFiles.get(id))] }));
   121	}
   122	
   123	// Collect scenarios from spec files.
   124	// → { byId, unidentified:[[file,title]], files, missingTargets:[], duplicates:[{id,files}] }
   125	// (+ failure when the matcher's channel fails — callers fail closed on it)
   126	function collectScenarios(specTargets, idRe, matcher) {
   127	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   128	  const missingTargets = specTargets.filter((t) => !fs.existsSync(t));
   129	  const files = [...new Set(specTargets.flatMap(mdFiles))].sort();
   130	  const pairs = [];
   131	  for (const f of files) scanTitles(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'), f, pairs);
   132	  const acc = bindPairs(pairs, m);
   133	  if (acc.failure) return { failure: acc.failure, byId: new Map(), unidentified: [], files, missingTargets, duplicates: [] };
   134	  return { byId: acc.byId, unidentified: acc.unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates: dupsOf(acc) };
   135	}
   136	
   137	// Same collection over in-memory texts (the projection path) — label = store-relative suffix.
   138	function collectScenariosFromTexts(texts, idRe, matcher) {
   139	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   140	  const pairs = [];
   141	  for (const [label, text] of texts) scanTitles(text, label, pairs);
   142	  const acc = bindPairs(pairs, m);
   143	  if (acc.failure) return { failure: acc.failure, byId: new Map(), unidentified: [], files: [...texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: [] };
   144	  return { byId: acc.byId, unidentified: acc.unidentified, files: [...texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: dupsOf(acc) };
   145	}
   146	
   147	// ---- TAP lexer (tap-contract): TAP is a version-aware protocol, not a pile of regexes ----
   148	const PLAN_LINE_RE = /^1\.\.(\d+)\s*(?:#.*)?$/;
   149	const POINT_LINE_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)(?:\s|$)/;
   150	const POINT_NUM_RE = /^(?:ok|not ok)\s+(\d+)(?:\s|$)/;   // complete numeric token only — `ok 1abc` stays unnumbered
   151	const SUPPORTED_TAP_VERSIONS = new Set([12, 13, 14]);
   152	
   153	// split at the first UNESCAPED '#' (backslash escapes the next char); decode \\ and \# in the
   154	// description ONLY (other backslash pairs stay literal) — so \# is never a directive delimiter
   155	function splitDirective(rest) {
   156	  let desc = '', directive = null;
   157	  for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) {
   158	    const c = rest[i];
   159	    if (c === '\\' && i + 1 < rest.length) { desc += c + rest[i + 1]; i++; continue; }
   160	    if (c === '#') { directive = rest.slice(i + 1); break; }
   161	    desc += c;
   162	  }
   163	  const isSkipTodo = directive !== null && /^\s*(skip|todo)/i.test(directive);
   164	  const decoded = desc.replace(/\\([\\#])/g, '$1').trim();
   165	  return { desc: decoded, isSkipTodo };
   166	}
   167	
   168	// does a text carry TAP-shaped tokens? (misrouted-channel detection — bail-out included)
   169	function looksLikeTap(text) {
   170	  return /^(?:TAP version |(?:not )?ok\b|1\.\.\d)/m.test(text || '') || /^\s*bail out!/im.test(text || '');
   171	}
   172	
   173	// Lex normalized stdout into typed top-level tokens. Line endings normalize first
   174	// (CRLF and lone-CR both end a line — a failure never hides behind a line-ending style).
   175	function lexTap(stdout) {
   176	  const lines = stdout.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n');
   177	  const tokens = [], problems = [];
   178	  let yamlOpen = -1;
   179	  for (let idx = 0; idx < lines.length; idx++) {
   180	    const line = lines[idx], n = idx + 1;
   181	    if (yamlOpen >= 0) {                                   // inside a top-level YAML block: opaque
   182	      if (line === '...') yamlOpen = -1;
   183	      continue;
   184	    }
   185	    if (/^\s*bail out!/i.test(line) && line.trim()) {      // case-insensitive, indentation-blind
   186	      tokens.push({ kind: 'bailout', line: line.trim(), n }); continue;
   187	    }
   188	    if (/^\s/.test(line)) continue;                        // indented = nested subtest detail
   189	    if (line === '---') { yamlOpen = n; continue; }
   190	    if (line.startsWith('TAP version ')) {
   191	      const m = /^TAP version (\d+)$/.exec(line);
   192	      tokens.push({ kind: 'version', num: m ? Number(m[1]) : null, raw: line, n }); continue;
   193	    }
   194	    let m;
   195	    if ((m = PLAN_LINE_RE.exec(line))) { tokens.push({ kind: 'plan', total: Number(m[1]), n }); continue; }
   196	    if (/^pragma [+-]\w+$/.test(line)) continue;           // the ONLY ignored TAP-14 construct
   197	    if (POINT_LINE_RE.test(line)) {
   198	      const fail = line.startsWith('not ok');
   199	      const nm = POINT_NUM_RE.exec(line);
   200	      let rest = line.slice(fail ? 'not ok'.length : 'ok'.length);
   201	      if (nm) rest = rest.replace(/^\s+\d+/, '');
   202	      rest = rest.replace(/^\s+/, '');
   203	      const { desc, isSkipTodo } = splitDirective(rest);
   204	      const description = desc.replace(/^-\s+/, '');       // the dash separator is optional (TAP)
   205	      tokens.push({ kind: 'point', fail, num: nm ? Number(nm[1]) : null, description, isSkipTodo, line, n });
   206	      continue;
   207	    }
   208	    // anything else at column 0 is diagnostics (comments, prose) — including `ok:`-style prefixes
   209	  }
   210	  if (yamlOpen >= 0) problems.push(`unterminated YAML diagnostic block opened at line ${yamlOpen} — a stray --- must not swallow the rest of the stream`);
   211	  return { tokens, problems };
   212	}
   213	
   214	// Parse TAP stdout → { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   215	//                      plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems }
   216	// (+ matchFailure when the matcher's channel fails — callers fail closed on it)
   217	// A # SKIP / # TODO directive is neither pass nor fail — a skipped test proves nothing.
   218	function parseTap(out, idRe, matcher) {
   219	  const results = new Map();
   220	  const untagged = [];
   221	  const unattributedFailures = [];
   222	  const { tokens, problems } = lexTap(out);
   223	  const tapProblems = [...problems];
   224	
   225	  // version discipline: at most one, leading, exact digits, inside the closed matrix
   226	  const versions = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'version');
   227	  const firstBody = tokens.find((t) => t.kind !== 'version');
   228	  if (versions.length > 1) tapProblems.push('multiple TAP version lines — one version promise per stream');
   229	  for (const v of versions) {
   230	    if (v.num === null) { tapProblems.push(`unparsable TAP version line at line ${v.n}: ${v.raw}`); continue; }
   231	    if (!SUPPORTED_TAP_VERSIONS.has(v.num)) tapProblems.push(`TAP version ${v.num} is not supported (supported: 12, 13, 14, or no version line)`);
   232	    if (firstBody && v.n > firstBody.n) tapProblems.push(`TAP version line at line ${v.n} arrives after the stream began — the version must lead`);
   233	  }
   234	
   235	  const bail = tokens.find((t) => t.kind === 'bailout');
   236	  const bailout = bail ? bail.line : null;
   237	
   238	  const pts = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'point');
   239	  const plans = tokens.filter((t) => t.kind === 'plan');
   240	  if (plans.length === 1) {
   241	    const plan = plans[0];
   242	    if (pts.some((p) => p.n < plan.n) && pts.some((p) => p.n > plan.n))
   243	      tapProblems.push(`TAP plan at line ${plan.n} sits mid-stream (points before and after) — a plan promises the whole stream`);
   244	    for (const p of pts) if (p.num !== null && (p.num < 1 || p.num > plan.total))
   245	      tapProblems.push(`test point number ${p.num} is outside the plan range 1..${plan.total}`);
   246	  }
   247	  const numSeen = new Set(), dupSet = new Set();
   248	  for (const p of pts) if (p.num !== null) { if (numSeen.has(p.num)) dupSet.add(p.num); else numSeen.add(p.num); }
   249	  const dupNumbers = [...dupSet].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   250	
   251	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   252	  const batch = m.batch(pts.map((p) => p.description));
   253	  if (batch.failure) {
   254	    return { results, untagged, untaggedFails: 0, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   255	      plans: plans.map((p) => p.total), points: pts.length, dupNumbers, tapProblems,
   256	      matchFailure: batch.failure };
   257	  }
   258	  pts.forEach((p, i) => {
   259	    const id = batch.ids[i];
   260	    if (id !== null) {
   261	      const cur = results.get(id) || { pass: 0, fail: 0, skip: 0 };
   262	      if (p.isSkipTodo) cur.skip++;
   263	      else cur[p.fail ? 'fail' : 'pass']++;
   264	      results.set(id, cur);
   265	    } else if (p.fail && !p.isSkipTodo) {
   266	      unattributedFailures.push(p.line);
   267	    } else {
   268	      untagged.push(p.description);
   269	    }
   270	  });
   271	  const untaggedFails = unattributedFailures.length;        // kept exported and compatible — doctor consumes it
   272	  return { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout,
   273	    plans: plans.map((p) => p.total), points: pts.length, dupNumbers, tapProblems };
   274	}
   275	
   276	// true when the test command produced output but not a single TAP result line was parsed —
   277	// almost always means the runner used a human reporter (node: add --test-reporter=tap)
   278	function zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures = []) {
   279	  if (results.size > 0 || untagged.length > 0 || unattributedFailures.length > 0) return false;
   280	  if (!out.trim()) return false;
   281	  // a TAP version line or plan (1..N) proves the reporter IS TAP — an empty suite is not a reporter problem
   282	  if (/^TAP version \d+|^1\.\.\d+/m.test(out)) return false;
   283	  return true;
   284	}
   285	
   286	function runTestCommand(cmd, cwd) {
   287	  const r = spawnSync(cmd, { shell: true, cwd, encoding: 'utf8' });
   288	  return {
   289	    out: r.stdout || '',            // TAP parses from stdout ONLY (tap-contract)
   290	    stderr: r.stderr || '',         // a separate diagnostics channel, reported verbatim
   291	    status: r.status,               // null when killed by signal
   292	    signal: r.signal || null,
   293	    error: r.error ? String(r.error.message || r.error) : null,
   294	  };
   295	}
   296	
   297	// Pure evaluation: cross-reference scenarios vs results → verdict object.
   298	// A scenario whose only results are skips is UNBOUND — skips prove nothing.
   299	function evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures = []) {
   300	  const specIds = [...byId.keys()].sort();
   301	  const boundGreen = [], boundRed = [], unbound = [], orphan = [];
   302	  for (const id of specIds) {
   303	    const r = results.get(id);
   304	    if (!r || (r.pass === 0 && r.fail === 0)) unbound.push(id);
   305	    else if (r.fail === 0) boundGreen.push(id);
   306	    else boundRed.push(id);
   307	  }
   308	  for (const id of [...results.keys()].sort()) if (!byId.has(id)) orphan.push(id);
   309	  const unattributed = unattributedFailures;
   310	  const clean = !boundRed.length && !unbound.length && !orphan.length && !unidentified.length && !unattributed.length;
   311	  return { boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan, unidentified, unattributed, clean };
   312	}
   313	
   314	// Fail-closed guard: reasons this run cannot be trusted regardless of the TAP picture.
   315	// Returns [] when the run is trustworthy.
   316	function infraErrors(run) {
   317	  const errs = [];
   318	  for (const t of run.missingTargets) errs.push(`spec target does not exist: ${t}`);
   319	  if (!run.missingTargets.length && run.fileCount === 0)
   320	    errs.push('no spec files found under the given targets — nothing to verify');
   321	  if (run.fileCount > 0 && run.scenarioCount === 0)
   322	    errs.push('spec files contain zero scenarios — nothing to verify');
   323	  if (run.exec.error) errs.push(`test command failed to spawn: ${run.exec.error}`);
   324	  if (run.exec.signal) errs.push(`test command was killed by signal ${run.exec.signal}`);
   325	  if (run.bailout) errs.push(`test run aborted: ${run.bailout}`);
   326	  for (const p of run.tapProblems || []) errs.push(`TAP protocol: ${p}`);
   327	  if (run.noTap)
   328	    errs.push('test command produced output but ZERO TAP results were parsed on stdout — the command is probably not emitting TAP (node test runner: add --test-reporter=tap; TAP on stderr: append 2>&1)');
   329	  // the TAP plan is a checked promise — defensive defaults keep legacy run shapes
   330	  // (direct callers of this exported helper) valid; only verify-created runs carry the fields
   331	  const plans = run.plans || [], points = run.points || 0, dupNumbers = run.dupNumbers || [];
   332	  if (plans.length > 1)
   333	    errs.push(`multiple TAP plans in one run (${plans.map((n) => `1..${n}`).join(', ')}) — cannot attribute test points to plans; run one TAP stream per verify (split the test command)`);
   334	  else if (plans.length === 1 && plans[0] !== points)
   335	    errs.push(`TAP plan declares ${plans[0]} test point(s) but ${points} were parsed — output truncated or garbled; refusing to trust this run`);
   336	  else if (plans.length === 1 && dupNumbers.length)
   337	    errs.push(`duplicate TAP test-point number(s): ${dupNumbers.join(', ')}`);
   338	  if (run.exec.status !== null && run.exec.status !== 0 && run.failCount === 0)
   339	    errs.push(`test command exited with status ${run.exec.status} but no parsed TAP failure explains it — the TAP picture is incomplete or truncated; refusing to trust this run`);
   340	  return errs;
   341	}
   342	
   343	function formatReport(v, results, fileCount, extras = {}) {
   344	  const lines = [];
   345	  const specCount = v.boundGreen.length + v.boundRed.length + v.unbound.length;
   346	  const tagged = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0);
   347	  const skipped = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + (r.skip || 0), 0);
   348	  lines.push(`specs: ${fileCount} file(s), ${specCount} identified scenario(s)`);
   349	  lines.push(`tests: ${tagged} tagged result(s)${skipped ? `, ${skipped} skipped (not counted)` : ''}\n`);
   350	  const groups = [['BOUND-GREEN', v.boundGreen], ['BOUND-RED', v.boundRed],
   351	                 ['UNBOUND (scenario, no test)', v.unbound], ['ORPHAN (test, no scenario)', v.orphan]];
   352	  for (const [label, items] of groups) {
   353	    if (!items.length) continue;
   354	    lines.push(`${label === 'BOUND-GREEN' ? '✓' : '✗'} ${label}: ${items.length}`);
   355	    for (const id of items) {
   356	      const r = results.get(id);
   357	      lines.push(`    ${id}${r ? `  ${r.pass}p/${r.fail}f${r.skip ? `/${r.skip}s` : ''}` : ''}`);
   358	    }
   359	  }
   360	  if (v.unattributed && v.unattributed.length) {
   361	    lines.push(`✗ UNATTRIBUTED FAILURES (not ok without a scenario ID): ${v.unattributed.length}`);
   362	    for (const raw of v.unattributed.slice(0, 20))
   363	      lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   364	    if (v.unattributed.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${v.unattributed.length - 20} more`);
   365	  }
   366	  if (v.unidentified.length) {
   367	    lines.push(`✗ UNIDENTIFIED (scenario, no ID — unbindable): ${v.unidentified.length}`);
   368	    for (const [f, t] of v.unidentified) lines.push(`    ${path.basename(f)}: ${t.slice(0, 60)}`);
   369	  }
   370	  if (extras.duplicates && extras.duplicates.length) {
   371	    lines.push(`✗ DUPLICATE scenario IDs (ambiguous binding): ${extras.duplicates.length}`);
   372	    for (const d of extras.duplicates) lines.push(`    ${d.id}: ${d.files.map((f) => path.basename(f)).join(', ')}`);
   373	  }
   374	  if (extras.stderr && extras.stderr.trim()) {
   375	    const sl = extras.stderr.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n').filter((l) => l.length);
   376	    lines.push(`— STDERR DIAGNOSTICS (not TAP): ${sl.length} line(s)`);
   377	    for (const raw of sl.slice(0, 20)) lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   378	    if (sl.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${sl.length - 20} more`);
   379	  }
   380	  const errs = extras.errors || [];
   381	  lines.push('\nRESULT: ' + (errs.length ? 'ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)'
   382	    : (v.clean && !(extras.duplicates || []).length ? 'GREEN — spec is the test suite' : 'GAPS')));
   383	  return lines.join('\n');
   384	}
   385	
   386	// Projection inputs for `verify --change` (all failure classes → run.errors, exit 2).
   387	// Roots resolve against cwd exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
   388	function buildChangeProjection(change, cwd) {
   389	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   390	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   391	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   392	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   393	  if (d.errors.length) return { projection, errors: d.errors, texts: null };
   394	  const p = am.buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, change);
   395	  projection.modules = p.modules;
   396	  projection.conflicts = p.conflicts;
   397	  projection.unstampedMutations = p.unstampedMutations;      // WARN grade — cli prints, verdict untouched
   398	  projection.notes = p.notes;
   399	  const errors = [...p.validation, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches];
   400	  if (p.conflicts.length) errors.push(`projection has ${p.conflicts.length} merge conflict(s) — no trustworthy projection: ${p.conflicts.join('; ')}`);
   401	  return { projection, errors, texts: errors.length ? null : p.texts };
   402	}
   403	
   404	// an errors-only run shape shared by every pre-test early exit (failed projection,
   405	// invalid pattern, matcher-channel failure) — fail closed WITHOUT running any tests
   406	function errorsRun(errors, projection) {
   407	  const verdict = evaluate(new Map(), [], new Map(), []);
   408	  const run = { verdict, results: new Map(), fileCount: 0, failCount: 0, scenarioCount: 0,
   409	    missingTargets: [], duplicates: [], bailout: null, noTap: false, tapProblems: [], stderr: '',
   410	    exec: { status: null, signal: null, error: null }, errors };
   411	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   412	  return run;
   413	}
   414	
   415	// enumeration-only projection metadata for the invalid-pattern early exit on --change runs:
   416	// SR-23's projection contract survives, yet no delta CONTENT is read (directory listing only)
   417	function enumerateProjection(change, cwd) {
   418	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   419	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   420	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   421	  if (!d.errors.length) projection.modules = d.files.map((f) => f.suffix);
   422	  return projection;
   423	}
   424	
   425	// Full verify run. opts: { specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd }
   426	function verify(opts) {
   427	  const cwd = opts.cwd || '.';
   428	  // pattern resolution FIRST — before any spec content is read or any test command spawned
   429	  const resolved = resolveIdPattern(cwd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   430	  if (resolved.error)
   431	    return errorsRun([resolved.error], opts.change ? enumerateProjection(opts.change, cwd) : null);
   432	  const idRe = new RegExp(resolved.source);
   433	  const matcher = makeIdMatcher(resolved);
   434	  let collected, projection = null;
   435	  if (opts.change) {
   436	    const b = buildChangeProjection(opts.change, cwd);
   437	    projection = b.projection;
   438	    if (!b.texts) return errorsRun(b.errors, projection);
   439	    collected = collectScenariosFromTexts(b.texts, idRe, matcher);
   440	  } else {
   441	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   442	  }
   443	  if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   444	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   445	  const exec = runTestCommand(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   446	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   447	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   448	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   449	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   450	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   451	    return run;
   452	  }
   453	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   454	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   455	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   456	  const run = {
   457	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   458	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   459	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   460	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   461	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   462	    tapProblems,
   463	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   464	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   465	  };
   466	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   467	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   468	  return run;
   469	}
   470	
   471	// machine-consumable shape for a verify run
   472	function verifyJson(run) {
   473	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   474	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 && run.errors.length === 0;
   475	  const json = projectionless(run, clean);
   476	  if (run.projection) json.projection = run.projection;   // ONLY --change runs carry this field
   477	  return json;
   478	}
   479	
   480	function projectionless(run, clean) {
   481	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   482	  return {
   483	    clean,
   484	    result: run.errors.length ? 'ERROR' : (clean ? 'GREEN' : 'GAPS'),
   485	    errors: run.errors,
   486	    specFiles: run.fileCount,
   487	    exec: run.exec,
   488	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   489	    boundGreen: v.boundGreen.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   490	    boundRed: v.boundRed.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   491	    unbound: v.unbound,
   492	    orphan: v.orphan.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   493	    unidentified: v.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })),
   494	    unattributedFailures: { count: (v.unattributed || []).length, lines: v.unattributed || [] },
   495	    stderr: run.stderr || '',
   496	  };
   497	}
   498	
   499	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   500	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   501	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   502	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   503	  return value;
   504	}
   505	
   506	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row;\n --id-pattern may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has an id-pattern row)';
   507	
   508	function cli(argv) {
   509	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   510	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   511	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   512	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   513	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   514	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   515	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   516	      return 2;
   517	    }
   518	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   519	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   520	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   521	      a.testCmd = c;
   522	    }
   523	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   524	    const run = verify(a);
   525	    if (run.projection)
   526	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   527	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   528	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   529	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   530	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr }));
   531	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   532	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   533	  });
   534	}
   535	
   536	module.exports = { leadId, stripFences, collectScenarios, collectScenariosFromTexts, parseTap, looksLikeTap,
   537	  zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, evaluate, infraErrors, formatReport, verifyJson, verify,
   538	  configTestCmd, cli, DEFAULT_ID, makeIdMatcher, _setChildRunner };
