     2	/*
     3	 * apriori doctor — diagnose the project↔apriori seam. Seven checks, one exit code:
     4	 * 0 = HEALTHY · 1 = FINDINGS · 2 = UNUSABLE (Node floor, uninitialized, usage error).
     5	 * Doctor never repairs and never writes; its ONLY side effect is D5 running the
     6	 * project's test command once (--no-run removes even that). Findings name their fixer.
     7	 */
     8	const fs = require('fs');
     9	const path = require('path');
    10	const { TOOLS, detectTools } = require('./init');
    11	const { checkRunbookFreshness } = require('./check');
    12	const { collectScenarios, parseTap, zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, configTestCmd, DEFAULT_ID, looksLikeTap, makeIdMatcher } = require('./spec-runner');
    13	const { resolveIdPattern } = require('./config');
    14	const { activeChanges, parseFlowState } = require('./status');
    15	const { containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');
    16	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    17	const { legacyRoots } = require('./resolve');
    18	
    19	const NODE_FLOOR = 22;
    20	
    21	// type-honest probes: doctor's whole job is malformed scaffolds — a file impersonating a
    22	// directory (or vice versa) must become a FINDING, never a false pass or a crash
    23	function isDir(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; } }
    24	function isFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile(); } catch { return false; } }
    25	function readIfFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile() ? fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8') : null; } catch { return null; } }
    26	
    27	// D5's classifier over a runTestCommand result — exported for the Windows-safe signal test (DR-06)
    28	function classifyProbe(run) {
    29	  // run = runTestCommand's shape: { out, status, signal, error }
    30	  const { out } = run;
    31	  if (run.error) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command failed to spawn: ${run.error}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    32	  if (run.signal) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command was killed by signal ${run.signal}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    33	  const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID);
    34	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, tapProblems } = parseTap(out, idRe);
    35	  if (bailout) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test run aborted: ${bailout}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    36	  // the lexer's protocol problems (unsupported version, YAML, plan placement) are seam findings (DR-14)
    37	  if (tapProblems && tapProblems.length)
    38	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `TAP protocol: ${tapProblems[0]}`, fix: 'emit supported TAP (12/13/14 or no version line) from the test command' };
    39	  // TAP on stderr with a silent stdout: the channel is misrouted, not missing (DR-14)
    40	  if (!out.trim() && looksLikeTap(run.stderr))
    41	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP arrives on stderr while stdout is silent — verify reads stdout only', fix: 'append 2>&1 to the test command' };
    42	  if (!out.trim()) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'test command produced no output', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    43	  if (zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures))
    44	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'output is not TAP — node test runner needs --test-reporter=tap', fix: 'emit TAP from the test command' };
    45	  const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0) + untagged.length + unattributedFailures.length;
    46	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
    47	  // an unexplained non-zero exit is classified BEFORE any ok branch — a non-zero `1..0` run lands here
    48	  if (run.status !== null && run.status !== 0 && failCount === 0)
    49	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command exited ${run.status} unexplained by TAP — truncated?`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    50	  if (parsed === 0) {
    51	    if (/^1\.\.0\b/m.test(out)) return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: 'TAP plumbing OK (empty suite, 1..0)' };
    52	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP stream truncated or malformed (version/plan but zero result lines)', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    53	  }
    54	  const pass = parsed - failCount;
    55	  return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: `TAP plumbing OK (${pass}p/${failCount}f — test failures are verify's business, not doctor's)` };
    56	}
    57	
    58	// The whole diagnosis. → { code, result, findings, checks, errors }
    59	function runDoctor(opts = {}) {
    60	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
    61	  const checks = [], errors = [];
    62	  const finish = (code) => {
    63	    const findings = checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'finding').length;
    64	    const result = code === 2 ? 'UNUSABLE' : (findings ? 'FINDINGS' : 'HEALTHY');
    65	    return { code: code !== undefined ? code : (findings ? 1 : 0), result, findings, checks, errors };
    66	  };
    67	
    68	  // D1 — Node floor (version injectable for tests, DR-12)
    69	  const major = parseInt((opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node), 10);
    70	  if (major < NODE_FLOOR) {
    71	    checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'finding', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} is below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`, fix: 'upgrade Node' });
    72	    errors.push(`Node below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`);
    73	    return finish(2);
    74	  }
    75	  checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'ok', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} >= ${NODE_FLOOR}` });
    76	
    77	  // D2 — init scaffold
    78	  const ap = path.join(cwd, 'apriori');
    79	  if (!isDir(ap)) {
    80	    checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail: `no apriori/ here — not an apriori project yet`, fix: `run 'apriori init'` });
    81	    errors.push(`no apriori/ directory — run 'apriori init'`);
    82	    return finish(2);
    83	  }
    84	  const gaps = [];
    85	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md'))) gaps.push([`apriori/runbook.md ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')) ? 'is not a regular file' : 'missing'}`, `apriori init`]);
    86	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'specs'))) gaps.push([`apriori/specs/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'specs')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'missing (half-initialized scaffold)'}`, `apriori init`]);
    87	  const giText = readIfFile(path.join(ap, '.gitignore'));
    88	  if (giText === null || !/^tmp\/$/m.test(giText)) gaps.push(['apriori/.gitignore missing, unreadable, or missing its tmp/ line', `apriori update`]);
    89	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'tmp'))) gaps.push([`apriori/tmp/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'tmp')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'dir missing'}`, `apriori update / mkdir`]);
    90	  if (gaps.length) for (const [detail, fix] of gaps) checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail, fix });
    91	  else checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'ok', detail: 'scaffold complete' + (fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'process-config.md')) ? '' : ' (no process-config.md — optional, defaults apply)') });
    92	
    93	  // D3 — runbook freshness (absence OR wrong type is D2's finding; D3 must never contradict or crash)
    94	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')))
    95	    checks.push({ id: 'D3', status: 'n/a', detail: 'runbook missing or not a regular file — see D2; freshness not checkable' });
    96	  else {
    97	    const warn = checkRunbookFreshness(cwd);
    98	    checks.push(warn.length
    99	      ? { id: 'D3', status: 'finding', detail: warn[0], fix: 'apriori update' }
   100	      : { id: 'D3', status: 'ok', detail: 'runbook copy matches the installed CLI' });
   101	  }
   102	
   103	  // D4 — tool pointers for DETECTED tools only
   104	  const detected = detectTools(cwd);
   105	  if (!detected.length) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no known AI-tool markers detected' });
   106	  else {
   107	    let broken = 0;
   108	    for (const key of detected) {
   109	      const t = TOOLS[key];
   110	      const rules = path.join(cwd, t.rules);
   111	      const rulesText = readIfFile(rules);
   112	      if (rulesText === null || !rulesText.includes('apriori/runbook.md')) {
   113	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: ${t.rules} ${rulesText !== null ? 'lost its runbook pointer' : (fs.existsSync(rules) ? 'is not a regular readable file' : 'is missing')}`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   114	        broken++;
   115	      }
   116	      if (t.command && !isFile(path.join(cwd, t.command))) {
   117	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: command file ${t.command} is missing`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   118	        broken++;
   119	      }
   120	    }
   121	    if (!broken) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'ok', detail: `pointers intact for: ${detected.join(', ')}` });
   122	  }
   123	
   124	  // D6 is COMPUTED before the D5 probe (the id-pattern must be validated — and its store
   125	  // scan survived — before any test command runs; display order stays D5 then D6): an
   126	  // uncompilable or terminated config pattern skips the probe entirely.
   127	  const specsDir = path.join(ap, 'specs');
   128	  const d6Entries = [];
   129	  let idPatternBroken = false;
   130	  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, null);
   131	  if (idp.error) {
   132	    idPatternBroken = true;
   133	    d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: idp.error, fix: 'fix the id-pattern row in apriori/process-config.md' });
   134	  } else if (!fs.existsSync(specsDir)) d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no store dir — see D2' });
   135	  else {
   136	    const collected = collectScenarios([specsDir], new RegExp(idp.source), makeIdMatcher(idp));
   137	    if (collected.failure) {
   138	      idPatternBroken = true;
   139	      d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `process-config id-pattern matching failed (${collected.failure})`, fix: 'fix the id-pattern row in apriori/process-config.md' });
   140	    } else {
   141	      const { byId, unidentified, files, duplicates } = collected;
   142	      const src = ` (id-pattern: ${idp.origin})`;
   143	      if (!files.length || (byId.size === 0 && unidentified.length === 0))
   144	        d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'empty store — normal for a new project' });
   145	      else {
   146	        let bad = 0;
   147	        if (unidentified.length) { d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `scenario(s) without a bindable ${idp.source} ID${src}: ${unidentified.map(([, t]) => t.slice(0, 40)).join(' · ')}`, fix: 'add leading IDs' }); bad++; }
   148	        if (duplicates.length) { d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `duplicate scenario IDs: ${duplicates.map((d) => d.id).join(', ')}`, fix: 'deduplicate IDs' }); bad++; }
   149	        if (!bad) d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'ok', detail: `${byId.size} scenario(s), all bindable, no duplicates${src}` });
   150	      }
   151	    }
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  // D5 — TAP probe (the one declared side effect; --no-run and a broken id-pattern skip)
   155	  const cfgCmd = opts.testCmd ? null : configTestCmd(cwd);
   156	  const testCmd = opts.testCmd || (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error ? null : cfgCmd);
   157	  if (idPatternBroken) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (invalid id-pattern config)' });
   158	  else if (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `config: ${cfgCmd.error}`, fix: 'keep one live test-cmd row' });
   159	  else if (!testCmd) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no test command configured — pass --test-cmd or set one via apriori init --test-cmd' });
   160	  else if (opts.noRun) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (--no-run)' });
   161	  else checks.push(classifyProbe(runTestCommand(testCmd, cwd)));
   162	
   163	  // D6 — store health (computed above, displayed here)
   164	  checks.push(...d6Entries);
   165	
   166	  // D7 — changes overview
   167	  const actives = activeChanges(cwd);
   168	  const notes = [];
   169	  let d7bad = 0;
   170	  const changesRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   171	  for (const c of actives) {
   172	    const fp = path.join(changesRoot, c, 'flow-state.md');
   173	    if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: no flow-state.md`, fix: 'apriori new / restore the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   174	    // per-file containment: a symlinked flow-state pointing outside the changes tree is
   175	    // diagnosed, never read (doctor must not leak outside content into its report)
   176	    if (!containsReal(changesRoot, fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state.md escapes apriori/changes/ (symlink?) — not read`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   177	    let st;
   178	    try { st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8')); }
   179	    catch (e) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state unreadable (${e.message})`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   180	    if (!st.change) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state has no 'change:' key`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   181	    else if (st.change !== c) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state says 'change: ${st.change}' — mismatches the dir`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   182	    else notes.push(`${c} @ ${st['current-step'] || '?'}`);
   183	  }
   184	  const archRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive');
   185	  if (fs.existsSync(archRoot)) {
   186	    for (const b of fs.readdirSync(archRoot).sort()) {
   187	      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-/.test(b)) continue;
   188	      const dir = path.join(archRoot, b);
   189	      let isDir = false;
   190	      try { isDir = fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory(); } catch { continue; }
   191	      if (!isDir) continue;
   192	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, dir)) { notes.push(`${b}: skipped (escapes archive/ — symlink?)`); continue; }
   193	      const fp = path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md');
   194	      if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) continue;
   195	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, fp)) { notes.push(`${b}: flow-state escapes archive/ (symlink?) — not read`); continue; }
   196	      const st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8'));
   197	      const step = st['current-step'];
   198	      if (step && step !== 'DONE' && step !== 'ABANDONED') notes.push(`archived ${b} @ ${step} — gate ④ possibly pending`);
   199	    }
   200	  }
   201	  if (!d7bad) checks.push(actives.length || notes.length
   202	    ? { id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') || `${actives.length} active change(s), flow-states valid` }
   203	    : { id: 'D7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no changes yet' });
   204	  else if (notes.length) checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') });
   205	
   206	  // D8 — legacy 3.x layout roots (existence-level, never followed or read through)
   207	  const legacy = legacyRoots(cwd);
   208	  checks.push(legacy.length
   209	    ? { id: 'D8', status: 'finding', detail: `legacy 3.x layout root(s) present: ${legacy.join(', ')}`, fix: 'migrate the artifacts into their change bundles — see MIGRATING.md (4.0): https://github.com/Apriorhythm/apriori-spec-development/blob/main/MIGRATING.md' }
   210	    : { id: 'D8', status: 'ok', detail: 'no legacy 3.x layout roots' });
   211	
   212	  return finish();
   213	}
   214	
   215	function toJson(res) {
   216	  return { result: res.result, findings: res.findings, checks: res.checks, errors: res.errors };
   217	}
   218	
   219	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori doctor [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--no-run] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]';
   220	
   221	function cli(argv) {
   222	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'doctor', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   223	    flags: { '--test-cmd': 'value', '--no-run': 'flag', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' },
   224	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ result: 'UNUSABLE', findings: 0, checks: [], errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   225	    const a = { testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null, noRun: !!f['--no-run'], cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'] };
   226	    const res = runDoctor(a);
   227	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   228	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('doctor: ' + e);
   229	    const mark = { ok: '✓', finding: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
   230	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.detail}${c.fix ? `  → ${c.fix}` : ''}`);
   231	    console.log(res.code === 2 ? '\nDOCTOR: UNUSABLE (see errors)'
   232	      : res.findings ? `\nDOCTOR: ${res.findings} finding(s)` : '\nDOCTOR: HEALTHY');
   233	    return res.code;
   234	  });
   235	}
   236	
   237	module.exports = { runDoctor, classifyProbe, cli };
   280	}
   281	
   282	// ---- cli --------------------------------------------------------------------
   283	// Two modes. CONSUMER (default): checks the project's own spec store (CK-04) and
   284	// scaffolded-runbook freshness (CK-06) — nothing else, so a consumer legitimately using
   285	// OpenSpec or shipping its own README is never failed by apriori's self-checks.
   286	// SELF (--self): additionally runs the apriori repo's handbook checks (EN/CN pairs,
   287	// verdict phrases, codex commands, no-openspec residue) — used by this repo's own CI.
   288	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori check [--specs <dir>] [--self]';
   289	
   290	function cli(argv) {
   291	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'check', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   292	    flags: { '--specs': 'value', '--self': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   293	    const root = process.cwd();
   294	    const specsDir = f['--specs'] || 'apriori/specs';
   295	    const self = !!f['--self'];
   296	  const fails = [];
   297	
   298	    // CK-04 over the spec store — a missing path is an ERROR, never a silent PASS
   299	    const specsPath = path.join(root, specsDir);
   300	    if (!fs.existsSync(specsPath)) {
   301	      console.error(`error: spec store path does not exist: ${specsDir}` +
   302	        (fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'apriori')) ? '' : ` — is this an apriori project? (run 'apriori init')`));
   303	      console.log('\nRESULT: ERROR');
   304	      return 2;
   305	    }
   306	    const specFiles = [];
   307	    (function walk(d) {
   308	      for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   309	        const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   310	        if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p); else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) specFiles.push(p);
   311	      }
   312	    })(specsPath);
   313	    // CK-04 consumes the project's id-pattern (config row > default; check has no flag) and
   314	    // batches through the shared matcher — a broken or pathological row is RESULT: ERROR (CK-15/16)
   315	    const resolved = resolveIdPattern(root, null);
   316	    if (resolved.error) {
   317	      console.error('error: ' + resolved.error);
   318	      console.log('\nRESULT: ERROR');
   319	      return 2;
   320	    }
   321	    const pairs = [];
   322	    for (const f of specFiles) {
   323	      const rel = path.relative(root, f);
   324	      for (const t of scenarioTitles(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'))) pairs.push([rel, t]);
   325	    }
   326	    const batch = makeIdMatcher(resolved).batch(pairs.map(([, t]) => t));
   327	    if (batch.failure) {
   328	      console.error(`error: process-config id-pattern matching failed (${batch.failure})`);
   329	      console.log('\nRESULT: ERROR');
   330	      return 2;
   331	    }
   332	    pairs.forEach(([rel, t], i) => {
   333	      if (batch.ids[i] === null) fails.push(`${rel}: scenario without a bindable ID: ${t.slice(0, 50)}`);
   334	    });
   335	
   336	    // CK-10: review-evidence secret tripwire (consumer mode; absent dir skips)
   337	    const secrets = checkReviewSecrets(root);
   338	    fails.push(...secrets.fails);
   339	    for (const w of secrets.warns) console.log('! ' + w);
   340	
   341	    if (self) {
   342	      // Handbook checks (apriori repo dogfooding) — run when the pairs exist.
   343	      // CK-08: a ONE-SIDED pair fails (a missing mirror must not skip silently);
   344	      // both sides absent → the pair is skipped (older checkouts unaffected).
   345	      const PAIRS = [['README.md', 'README_cn.md'], ['RUNBOOK.md', 'RUNBOOK_cn.md'], ['VISION.md', 'VISION_cn.md'],
   346	        ['docs/concepts.md', 'docs/concepts_cn.md'], ['docs/legacy.md', 'docs/legacy_cn.md'],
   347	        ['docs/ci.md', 'docs/ci_cn.md'], ['docs/cli.md', 'docs/cli_cn.md'],
   348	        ['docs/troubleshooting.md', 'docs/troubleshooting_cn.md']];
   349	      const files = {};
   350	      for (const [en, cn] of PAIRS) {
   351	        const pe = fs.existsSync(path.join(root, en)), pc = fs.existsSync(path.join(root, cn));
   352	        if (pe !== pc) fails.push(`one-sided EN/CN pair: ${pe ? cn : en} is missing`);
   353	        for (const nm of [en, cn]) {
   354	          const p = path.join(root, nm);
   355	          if (fs.existsSync(p)) files[nm] = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8');
   356	        }
   357	      }
   358	      for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(files)) {
   359	        fails.push(...checkLinks(path.dirname(path.join(root, name)), name, text));   // CK-09: per-file base
   360	        fails.push(...checkNoOpenspec(name, text));
   361	      }
   362	      for (const [en, cn] of PAIRS) if (files[en] && files[cn]) fails.push(...checkHeadingAlignment(en, files[en], cn, files[cn]));
   363	      if (Object.keys(files).length) fails.push(...checkVerdictPhrases(files));
   364	      if (files['RUNBOOK.md'] && files['RUNBOOK_cn.md']) fails.push(...checkCodexCommands(files['RUNBOOK.md'], files['RUNBOOK_cn.md']));
   365	      if (Object.keys(files).length) fails.push(...checkCodexKnownForms(files));
   366	      fails.push(...checkRunbookVersion(root, files));   // CK-11
   367	    }
   368	
   369	    for (const f of fails) console.log('✗ ' + f);
   370	    for (const w of checkRunbookFreshness(root)) console.log('! ' + w);   // CK-06: warn-only
   371	    console.log('\nRESULT:', fails.length ? `FAIL (${fails.length})` : 'PASS');
   372	    return fails.length ? 1 : 0;
   373	  });
   374	}
   375	
   376	module.exports = {
   377	  ghSlug, headings, anchors, fileLinks, checkLinks, checkReviewSecrets, checkHeadingAlignment,
   378	  checkVerdictPhrases, codexCandidates, checkCodexCommands, checkCodexKnownForms, checkScenarioIds, checkNoOpenspec,
   379	  checkRunbookFreshness, checkRunbookVersion, cli,
   380	};
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori gate — aggregate the MECHANICAL exit conditions for one change into one exit code.
     4	 * 0 = PASS · 1 = BLOCKED · 2 = the evaluation itself is untrustworthy.
     5	 * Strictly read-only. PASS covers machine checks only — human gates remain human.
     6	 */
     7	const fs = require('fs');
     8	const path = require('path');
     9	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    10	const { parseFlowState, parseLedger } = require('./status');
    11	const { verify, configTestCmd } = require('./spec-runner');
    12	const { CHANGE_NAME_RE, containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');
    13	const { resolveChange, configCas, configCasProblem } = require('./resolve');
    14	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    15	
    16	const STEP_ENUM = ['STEP0', 'STEP1', 'STEP2', 'STEP3', 'STEP4', 'STEP5', 'STEP6',
    17	  'INTENT-CARD', 'SPIKE', 'EXTRACTION', 'DONE', 'ABANDONED'];
    18	const TIER_ENUM = ['trivial', 'medium', 'large'];
    19	const CAVEAT = 'mechanical checks only; human gates remain human';
    20	
    21	function err(res, msg) { res.errors.push(msg); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
    22	
    23	
    24	// --- individual checks; each returns {id, status: 'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a', detail} ---
    25	
    26	function checkFlowState(state, name) {
    27	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'lineage', 'current-step']) {
    28	    const v = state[key];
    29	    if (v === undefined || v === '') return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: required key '${key}' missing` };
    30	    if (v.includes('<') || v.includes('>')) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: '${key}' is an unfilled placeholder (${v})` };
    31	  }
    32	  if (state.change !== name) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'change' is '${state.change}', expected '${name}'` };
    33	  if (!STEP_ENUM.includes(state['current-step'])) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'current-step' '${state['current-step']}' not in the legal vocabulary` };
    34	  if (!TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier)) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'tier' '${state.tier}' not in {trivial, medium, large}` };
    35	  return { id: 'C3', status: 'pass', detail: `legal (tier ${state.tier}, ${state['current-step']})` };
    36	}
    37	
    38	function checkTasks(dir, tier) {
    39	  const p = path.join(dir, 'tasks.md');
    40	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    41	    return tier === 'trivial'
    42	      ? { id: 'C2', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no tasks.md — trivial tier has no STEP2' }
    43	      : { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md missing at ${p}` };
    44	  }
    45	  const open = (fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8').match(/^\s*-\s\[\s\]/gm) || []).length;
    46	  return open
    47	    ? { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md has ${open} unchecked box(es)` }
    48	    : { id: 'C2', status: 'pass', detail: 'all tasks checked' };
    49	}
    50	
    51	// the ledger vocabulary, one classifier shared by C4 and the corpus test (GT-15).
    52	// leading token, case-insensitive; rejected-verified BEFORE rejected (alternation order).
    53	const STATUS_RE = /^(open|fixed|verified|rejected-verified|rejected|advisory-acked|waived)\b/i;
    54	function classifyStatus(status) {
    55	  const m = STATUS_RE.exec(status);
    56	  if (!m) return { legal: false, terminal: false, needsReason: false, hasReason: false, isWaived: false };
    57	  const tok = m[1].toLowerCase();
    58	  const needsReason = tok === 'rejected' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived';
    59	  return {
    60	    legal: true,
    61	    terminal: tok === 'verified' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived' || tok === 'advisory-acked',
    62	    needsReason,
    63	    hasReason: !needsReason || /\w/.test(status.slice(m[0].length)),
    64	    isWaived: tok === 'waived',
    65	    token: tok,
    66	  };
    67	}
    68	
    69	const escapeRe = (s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    70	
    71	// gates: entries from a flow-state text — the block runs from the unindented `gates:` line
    72	// to the next unindented top-level key; entries start `- `, continuation lines attach.
    73	function gatesEntries(flowText) {
    74	  const start = flowText.search(/^gates:/m);
    75	  if (start < 0) return [];
    76	  const rest = flowText.slice(start + 'gates:'.length);
    77	  const end = rest.search(/^[A-Za-z][\w-]*:/m);
    78	  const block = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    79	  const entries = [];
    80	  for (const line of block.split('\n')) {
    81	    if (/^\s*-\s/.test(line)) entries.push(line.trim());
    82	    else if (entries.length && line.trim()) entries[entries.length - 1] += ' ' + line.trim();
    83	  }
    84	  return entries;
    85	}
    86	
    87	// a waive is a HUMAN act: one single gates: entry must carry the row ID as an exact
    88	// token (LS-1 never matches inside LS-10) AND the word waive/waived.
    89	function waiveEvidence(flowText, id) {
    90	  const idRe = new RegExp(`(^|[^A-Za-z0-9-])${escapeRe(id)}([^A-Za-z0-9-]|$)`);
    91	  return gatesEntries(flowText).some((e) => idRe.test(e) && /waiv/i.test(e));
    92	}
    93	
    94	function checkLedger(tier, stage, dir) {
    95	  const p = path.join(dir, 'review', 'issues.md');
    96	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    97	    return tier === 'trivial'
    98	      ? { id: 'C4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no ledger — trivial tier may never open one' }
    99	      : { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: `ledger missing at ${p}` };
   100	  }
   101	  const rows = parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
   102	  const fsPath = dir ? path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md') : null;
   103	  const flowText = fsPath && fs.existsSync(fsPath) ? fs.readFileSync(fsPath, 'utf8') : '';
   104	  const bad = [];
   105	  for (const r of rows) {
   106	    const c = classifyStatus(r.status);
   107	    if (!c.legal) { bad.push(`${r.id}: '${r.status}' is not in the ledger vocabulary`); continue; }
   108	    if (c.needsReason && !c.hasReason) { bad.push(`${r.id} is ${c.token} without a reason`); continue; }
   109	    if (c.isWaived && !waiveEvidence(flowText, r.id)) {
   110	      bad.push(`${r.id} is waived without a gates: entry recording the human decision`); continue;
   111	    }
   112	    if (c.token === 'open') { bad.push(`${r.id} is open`); continue; }
   113	    if (stage === 'archived' && !c.terminal) {
   114	      bad.push(c.token === 'fixed'
   115	        ? `${r.id} is fixed but never verified — reviewer must verify, or the human waives it`
   116	        : `${r.id} is rejected without reviewer concurrence — flip to rejected-verified, or the human waives it`);
   117	    }
   118	  }
   119	  return bad.length
   120	    ? { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: bad.join('; ') }
   121	    : { id: 'C4', status: 'pass', detail: `${rows.length} row(s), none blocking` };
   122	}
   123	
   124	// the bundle review/ dir is the shared evidence root for C4 and C5 — when present it must be
   125	// a REAL contained directory (symlinked/escaping/non-dir entries block, never read through)
   126	function reviewDirDefect(dir) {
   127	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   128	  let st;
   129	  try { st = fs.lstatSync(rd); } catch { return null; }  // lstat, not exists: a dangling symlink is a defect, not absence
   130	  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return `review is a symlink: ${rd}`;
   131	  if (!st.isDirectory()) return `review is not a directory: ${rd}`;
   132	  if (!containsReal(dir, rd)) return `review escapes the change dir: ${rd}`;
   133	  return null;
   134	}
   135	
   136	function checkEvidence(dir) {
   137	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   138	  const docs = [];
   139	  if (fs.existsSync(rd)) {
   140	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(rd, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   141	      if (!e.name.endsWith('.md') || e.name === 'issues.md' || /-raw(\.|$)/.test(e.name.replace(/\.md$/, ''))) continue;
   142	      if (e.name.includes('-raw')) continue;
   143	      const p = path.join(rd, e.name);
   144	      if (fs.lstatSync(p).isSymbolicLink())
   145	        return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `evidence doc is a symlink: ${e.name}` };
   146	      if (!fs.lstatSync(p).isFile()) continue;
   147	      docs.push(p);
   148	    }
   149	  }
   150	  const missing = [];
   151	  let verdictDocs = 0;
   152	  for (const doc of docs) {
   153	    if (!/^VERDICT:/m.test(fs.readFileSync(doc, 'utf8'))) continue;
   154	    verdictDocs++;
   155	    const stem = path.basename(doc).replace(/\.md$/, '');
   156	    const hasRaw = fs.existsSync(rd) && fs.readdirSync(rd).some((n) => {
   157	      if (!n.startsWith(stem + '-raw')) return false;
   158	      const rp = path.join(rd, n);
   159	      return fs.lstatSync(rp).isFile();
   160	    });
   161	    if (!hasRaw) missing.push(path.basename(doc));
   162	  }
   163	  if (missing.length) return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `verdict doc(s) without a raw archive: ${missing.join(', ')}` };
   164	  return { id: 'C5', status: 'pass', detail: `${verdictDocs} review doc(s), every verdict has raw evidence` };
   165	}
   166	
   167	function checkKb(cwd, dir) {
   168	  const specsDir = path.join(dir, 'specs');
   169	  const modules = new Set();
   170	  const walk = (d, base) => {
   171	    if (!fs.existsSync(d)) return;
   172	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   173	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   174	      if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p, base);
   175	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) modules.add(path.relative(base, p).split(path.sep)[0]);
   176	    }
   177	  };
   178	  walk(specsDir, specsDir);
   179	  if (!modules.size) return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no delta specs to map' };
   180	
   181	  // build the module → truth index (c6-truth-binding): a truth doc binds by its
   182	  // declared store-module (default = basename), never by a filename assumption
   183	  const truthDir = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'truth');
   184	  const index = new Map();                 // module → { text, sourceFiles, basename }
   185	  const conflicts = new Map();             // module → "declared by A and B"
   186	  if (fs.existsSync(truthDir)) {
   187	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(truthDir).sort()) {
   188	      if (!e.endsWith('.md')) continue;
   189	      const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(truthDir, e), 'utf8');
   190	      const h2 = text.search(/^##\s/m);
   191	      const header = h2 >= 0 ? text.slice(0, h2) : text;
   192	      const smM = header.match(/^store-module:\s*(.+)$/m);
   193	      const sfM = header.match(/^source-files:\s*(.+)$/m);
   194	      const mods = smM ? smM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : [e.replace(/\.md$/, '')];
   195	      const sourceFiles = sfM ? sfM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : null;   // null = default lib/<module>.js
   196	      for (const m of mods) {
   197	        if (index.has(m)) conflicts.set(m, `${m}: declared by both ${index.get(m).basename} and ${e}`);
   198	        else index.set(m, { text, sourceFiles, basename: e });
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	  }
   202	  const stripFencesKb = (t) => t.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, '');
   203	  const blocked = [], notes = [];
   204	  let checked = 0;
   205	  for (const m of [...modules].sort()) {
   206	    if (conflicts.has(m)) { blocked.push(conflicts.get(m)); continue; }
   207	    const entry = index.get(m);
   208	    if (!entry) { notes.push(`${m}: no truth doc`); continue; }
   209	    const noFence = stripFencesKb(entry.text);
   210	    const sc = noFence.match(/^source-commit:\s+(\S+)/m);
   211	    if (!sc) {
   212	      const attempt = noFence.split('\n').some((l) => l.includes('source-commit:') && !/^source-commit:\s+\S+/.test(l));
   213	      notes.push(attempt
   214	        ? `${m}: source-commit is not in the canonical fence-outside line-start form 'source-commit: <ref>'`
   215	        : `${m}: truth doc has no source-commit`);
   216	      continue;
   217	    }
   218	    const ref = sc[1];
   219	    const explicit = entry.sourceFiles !== null;
   220	    const declared = explicit ? entry.sourceFiles : [`lib/${m}.js`];
   221	    // an explicit declaration is a complete promise: every token must be verifiable
   222	    const usable = [];
   223	    let tokenBad = null;
   224	    for (const rel of declared) {
   225	      // an explicit token is a repo-relative promise: reject absolute/backslash/empty/. or .. segments
   226	      if (explicit && (rel === '' || path.isAbsolute(rel) || rel.includes('\\') ||
   227	          rel.split('/').some((seg) => seg === '' || seg === '.' || seg === '..'))) {
   228	        tokenBad = `${m}: malformed source-files token (must be a normalized repo-relative path): ${rel}`; break;
   229	      }
   230	      const abs = path.join(cwd, rel);
   231	      let st = null; try { st = fs.lstatSync(abs); } catch { /* absent */ }
   232	      if (!st) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token missing: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   233	      if (st.isSymbolicLink()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is a symlink: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   234	      if (!containsReal(cwd, abs)) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token escapes the repo: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   235	      if (!st.isFile() && !st.isDirectory()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is neither file nor directory: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   236	      usable.push(rel);
   237	    }
   238	    if (tokenBad) { blocked.push(tokenBad); continue; }
   239	    if (!usable.length) { notes.push(`${m}: no ${declared.join(' / ')} to compare`); continue; }
   240	    const g = spawnSync('git', ['-C', cwd, 'log', '--oneline', `${ref}..HEAD`, '--', ...usable], { encoding: 'utf8' });
   241	    if (g.error) { notes.push(`${m}: git unavailable (${g.error.message})`); continue; }
   242	    if (g.status !== 0) { notes.push(`${m}: git failed (${(g.stderr || '').split('\n')[0]})`); continue; }
   243	    const n = g.stdout.trim() ? g.stdout.trim().split('\n').length : 0;
   244	    if (n > 0) blocked.push(`${m}: ${n} commit(s) since ${ref}`);
   245	    else checked++;
   246	  }
   247	  if (blocked.length) return { id: 'C6', status: 'blocked', detail: blocked.join('; ') };
   248	  if (checked > 0) return { id: 'C6', status: 'pass', detail: `${checked} module stamp(s) up to date${notes.length ? `; ${notes.join('; ')}` : ''}` };
   249	  return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: notes.join('; ') };
   250	}
   251	
   252	function checkBinding(cwd, name, stage, testCmd, idPattern) {
   253	  const run = stage === 'in-flight'
   254	    ? verify({ change: name, cwd, testCmd, idPattern })
   255	    : verify({ specs: [path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs')], cwd, testCmd, idPattern });
   256	  if (run.errors.length) return { infra: run.errors };
   257	  const v = run.verdict;
   258	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0;
   259	  if (clean) return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'pass', detail: `verify GREEN (${stage})` }, projection: run.projection };
   260	  const parts = [];
   261	  for (const [label, arr] of [['red', v.boundRed], ['unbound', v.unbound], ['orphan', v.orphan], ['unidentified', v.unidentified], ['unattributed-failures', v.unattributed || []], ['duplicate-IDs', run.duplicates]])
   262	    if (arr.length) parts.push(`${arr.length} ${label}`);
   263	  return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'blocked', detail: `verify GAPS: ${parts.join(', ')}` }, projection: run.projection };
   264	}
   265	
   266	
   267	// C7: unstamped mutation deltas are denied by default — a waiver is always visible (GT-16).
   268	// Archived stage: the deltas are already merged; there is nothing left to stamp-check.
   269	function checkCas(projection, stage, noCas, cwd) {
   270	  if (stage === 'archived') return { id: 'C7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'deltas already merged' };
   271	  const um = (projection && projection.unstampedMutations) || [];
   272	  if (!um.length) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'every mutation delta is stamped' };  // nothing to consult — silent pass (GT-16)
   273	  if (noCas) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (--no-cas)' };
   274	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);                       // consumption-time only
   275	  if (problem) return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked', detail: `${problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver` };
   276	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (process-config cas: optional)' };
   277	  return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked',
   278	    detail: `unstamped mutation delta(s): ${um.join(', ')} — run: apriori stamp <store-file> (or waive with --no-cas / a process-config cas: optional row)` };
   279	}
   280	
   281	// The whole evaluation. → { code, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors }
   282	function runGate(opts) {
   283	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
   284	  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
   285	  if (!opts.change) return err(res, 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]');
   286	  const nameCheck = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(opts.change);
   287	  if (!nameCheck.ok) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' (${nameCheck.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`);
   288	  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
   289	  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
   290	  res.stage = loc.stage;
   291	  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
   292	  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
   293	  let state;
   294	  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
   295	  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
   296	  let testCmd = opts.testCmd;
   297	  if (!testCmd) {
   298	    const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
   299	    if (c && c.error) return err(res, `verify: ${c.error}`);
   300	    testCmd = c;
   301	  }
   302	  if (!testCmd) return err(res, 'no test command: pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to apriori/process-config.md');
   303	
   304	  const b = checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, testCmd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   305	  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
   306	  res.checks.push(b.check);
   307	  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
   308	  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
   309	  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
   310	  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
   311	  if (rdDefect) {
   312	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   313	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   314	  } else {
   315	    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
   316	    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
   317	  }
   318	
   319	  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
   320	  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));
   321	
   322	  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
   323	  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : 'PASS';
   324	  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : 0;
   325	  return res;
   326	}
   327	
   328	function toJson(res) {
   329	  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
   330	}
   331	
   332	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]';
   333	
   334	function cli(argv) {
   335	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   336	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   337	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   338	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   339	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (GT-24 empty flag)
   340	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   341	    const res = runGate(a);
   342	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   343	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   344	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
   345	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   346	    if (res.code !== 2) console.log(res.code === 0 ? `\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}` : `\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   347	    return res.code;
   348	  });
   349	}
   350	
   351	module.exports = { runGate, resolveChange, classifyStatus, cli };

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori doctor — diagnose the project↔apriori seam. Seven checks, one exit code:
     4	 * 0 = HEALTHY · 1 = FINDINGS · 2 = UNUSABLE (Node floor, uninitialized, usage error).
     5	 * Doctor never repairs and never writes; its ONLY side effect is D5 running the
     6	 * project's test command once (--no-run removes even that). Findings name their fixer.
     7	 */
     8	const fs = require('fs');
     9	const path = require('path');
    10	const { TOOLS, detectTools } = require('./init');
    11	const { checkRunbookFreshness } = require('./check');
    12	const { collectScenarios, parseTap, zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, configTestCmd, DEFAULT_ID, looksLikeTap, makeIdMatcher } = require('./spec-runner');

    21	// type-honest probes: doctor's whole job is malformed scaffolds — a file impersonating a
    22	// directory (or vice versa) must become a FINDING, never a false pass or a crash
    23	function isDir(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; } }
    24	function isFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile(); } catch { return false; } }
    25	function readIfFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile() ? fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8') : null; } catch { return null; } }

    27	// D5's classifier over a runTestCommand result — exported for the Windows-safe signal test (DR-06)
    28	function classifyProbe(run) {
    29	  // run = runTestCommand's shape: { out, status, signal, error }
    30	  const { out } = run;
    31	  if (run.error) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command failed to spawn: ${run.error}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    32	  if (run.signal) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command was killed by signal ${run.signal}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    33	  const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID);
    34	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, tapProblems } = parseTap(out, idRe);
    35	  if (bailout) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test run aborted: ${bailout}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    36	  // the lexer's protocol problems (unsupported version, YAML, plan placement) are seam findings (DR-14)
    37	  if (tapProblems && tapProblems.length)
    38	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `TAP protocol: ${tapProblems[0]}`, fix: 'emit supported TAP (12/13/14 or no version line) from the test command' };
    39	  // TAP on stderr with a silent stdout: the channel is misrouted, not missing (DR-14)
    40	  if (!out.trim() && looksLikeTap(run.stderr))
    41	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP arrives on stderr while stdout is silent — verify reads stdout only', fix: 'append 2>&1 to the test command' };
    42	  if (!out.trim()) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'test command produced no output', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    43	  if (zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures))
    44	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'output is not TAP — node test runner needs --test-reporter=tap', fix: 'emit TAP from the test command' };
    45	  const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0) + untagged.length + unattributedFailures.length;
    46	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
    47	  // an unexplained non-zero exit is classified BEFORE any ok branch — a non-zero `1..0` run lands here
    48	  if (run.status !== null && run.status !== 0 && failCount === 0)
    49	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command exited ${run.status} unexplained by TAP — truncated?`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    50	  if (parsed === 0) {
    51	    if (/^1\.\.0\b/m.test(out)) return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: 'TAP plumbing OK (empty suite, 1..0)' };
    52	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP stream truncated or malformed (version/plan but zero result lines)', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    53	  }
    54	  const pass = parsed - failCount;
    55	  return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: `TAP plumbing OK (${pass}p/${failCount}f — test failures are verify's business, not doctor's)` };
    56	}

    58	// The whole diagnosis. → { code, result, findings, checks, errors }
    59	function runDoctor(opts = {}) {
    60	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
    61	  const checks = [], errors = [];
    62	  const finish = (code) => {
    63	    const findings = checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'finding').length;
    64	    const result = code === 2 ? 'UNUSABLE' : (findings ? 'FINDINGS' : 'HEALTHY');
    65	    return { code: code !== undefined ? code : (findings ? 1 : 0), result, findings, checks, errors };
    66	  };
    67	
    68	  // D1 — Node floor (version injectable for tests, DR-12)
    69	  const major = parseInt((opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node), 10);
    70	  if (major < NODE_FLOOR) {
    71	    checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'finding', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} is below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`, fix: 'upgrade Node' });
    72	    errors.push(`Node below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`);
    73	    return finish(2);
    74	  }
    75	  checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'ok', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} >= ${NODE_FLOOR}` });
    76	
    77	  // D2 — init scaffold
    78	  const ap = path.join(cwd, 'apriori');
    79	  if (!isDir(ap)) {
    80	    checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail: `no apriori/ here — not an apriori project yet`, fix: `run 'apriori init'` });
    81	    errors.push(`no apriori/ directory — run 'apriori init'`);
    82	    return finish(2);
    83	  }
    84	  const gaps = [];
    85	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md'))) gaps.push([`apriori/runbook.md ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')) ? 'is not a regular file' : 'missing'}`, `apriori init`]);
    86	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'specs'))) gaps.push([`apriori/specs/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'specs')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'missing (half-initialized scaffold)'}`, `apriori init`]);
    87	  const giText = readIfFile(path.join(ap, '.gitignore'));
    88	  if (giText === null || !/^tmp\/$/m.test(giText)) gaps.push(['apriori/.gitignore missing, unreadable, or missing its tmp/ line', `apriori update`]);
    89	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'tmp'))) gaps.push([`apriori/tmp/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'tmp')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'dir missing'}`, `apriori update / mkdir`]);
    90	  if (gaps.length) for (const [detail, fix] of gaps) checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail, fix });
    91	  else checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'ok', detail: 'scaffold complete' + (fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'process-config.md')) ? '' : ' (no process-config.md — optional, defaults apply)') });
    92	
    93	  // D3 — runbook freshness (absence OR wrong type is D2's finding; D3 must never contradict or crash)
    94	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')))
    95	    checks.push({ id: 'D3', status: 'n/a', detail: 'runbook missing or not a regular file — see D2; freshness not checkable' });
    96	  else {
    97	    const warn = checkRunbookFreshness(cwd);
    98	    checks.push(warn.length
    99	      ? { id: 'D3', status: 'finding', detail: warn[0], fix: 'apriori update' }
   100	      : { id: 'D3', status: 'ok', detail: 'runbook copy matches the installed CLI' });
   101	  }
   102	
   103	  // D4 — tool pointers for DETECTED tools only
   104	  const detected = detectTools(cwd);
   105	  if (!detected.length) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no known AI-tool markers detected' });
   106	  else {
   107	    let broken = 0;
   108	    for (const key of detected) {
   109	      const t = TOOLS[key];
   110	      const rules = path.join(cwd, t.rules);
   111	      const rulesText = readIfFile(rules);
   112	      if (rulesText === null || !rulesText.includes('apriori/runbook.md')) {
   113	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: ${t.rules} ${rulesText !== null ? 'lost its runbook pointer' : (fs.existsSync(rules) ? 'is not a regular readable file' : 'is missing')}`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   114	        broken++;
   115	      }
   116	      if (t.command && !isFile(path.join(cwd, t.command))) {
   117	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: command file ${t.command} is missing`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   118	        broken++;
   119	      }
   120	    }
   121	    if (!broken) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'ok', detail: `pointers intact for: ${detected.join(', ')}` });
   122	  }
   123	
   124	  // D6 is COMPUTED before the D5 probe (the id-pattern must be validated — and its store
   125	  // scan survived — before any test command runs; display order stays D5 then D6): an
   126	  // uncompilable or terminated config pattern skips the probe entirely.
   127	  const specsDir = path.join(ap, 'specs');
   128	  const d6Entries = [];
   129	  let idPatternBroken = false;
   130	  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, null);
   131	  if (idp.error) {
   132	    idPatternBroken = true;
   133	    d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: idp.error, fix: 'fix the id-pattern row in apriori/process-config.md' });
   134	  } else if (!fs.existsSync(specsDir)) d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no store dir — see D2' });
   135	  else {
   136	    const collected = collectScenarios([specsDir], new RegExp(idp.source), makeIdMatcher(idp));
   137	    if (collected.failure) {
   138	      idPatternBroken = true;
   139	      d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `process-config id-pattern matching failed (${collected.failure})`, fix: 'fix the id-pattern row in apriori/process-config.md' });
   140	    } else {
   141	      const { byId, unidentified, files, duplicates } = collected;
   142	      const src = ` (id-pattern: ${idp.origin})`;
   143	      if (!files.length || (byId.size === 0 && unidentified.length === 0))
   144	        d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'empty store — normal for a new project' });
   145	      else {
   146	        let bad = 0;
   147	        if (unidentified.length) { d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `scenario(s) without a bindable ${idp.source} ID${src}: ${unidentified.map(([, t]) => t.slice(0, 40)).join(' · ')}`, fix: 'add leading IDs' }); bad++; }
   148	        if (duplicates.length) { d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `duplicate scenario IDs: ${duplicates.map((d) => d.id).join(', ')}`, fix: 'deduplicate IDs' }); bad++; }
   149	        if (!bad) d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'ok', detail: `${byId.size} scenario(s), all bindable, no duplicates${src}` });
   150	      }
   151	    }
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  // D5 — TAP probe (the one declared side effect; --no-run and a broken id-pattern skip)
   155	  const cfgCmd = opts.testCmd ? null : configTestCmd(cwd);
   156	  const testCmd = opts.testCmd || (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error ? null : cfgCmd);
   157	  if (idPatternBroken) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (invalid id-pattern config)' });
   158	  else if (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `config: ${cfgCmd.error}`, fix: 'keep one live test-cmd row' });
   159	  else if (!testCmd) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no test command configured — pass --test-cmd or set one via apriori init --test-cmd' });
   160	  else if (opts.noRun) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (--no-run)' });
   161	  else checks.push(classifyProbe(runTestCommand(testCmd, cwd)));
   162	
   163	  // D6 — store health (computed above, displayed here)
   164	  checks.push(...d6Entries);
   165	
   166	  // D7 — changes overview
   167	  const actives = activeChanges(cwd);
   168	  const notes = [];
   169	  let d7bad = 0;
   170	  const changesRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   171	  for (const c of actives) {
   172	    const fp = path.join(changesRoot, c, 'flow-state.md');
   173	    if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: no flow-state.md`, fix: 'apriori new / restore the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   174	    // per-file containment: a symlinked flow-state pointing outside the changes tree is
   175	    // diagnosed, never read (doctor must not leak outside content into its report)
   176	    if (!containsReal(changesRoot, fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state.md escapes apriori/changes/ (symlink?) — not read`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   177	    let st;
   178	    try { st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8')); }
   179	    catch (e) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state unreadable (${e.message})`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   180	    if (!st.change) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state has no 'change:' key`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   181	    else if (st.change !== c) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state says 'change: ${st.change}' — mismatches the dir`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   182	    else notes.push(`${c} @ ${st['current-step'] || '?'}`);
   183	  }
   184	  const archRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive');
   185	  if (fs.existsSync(archRoot)) {
   186	    for (const b of fs.readdirSync(archRoot).sort()) {
   187	      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-/.test(b)) continue;
   188	      const dir = path.join(archRoot, b);
   189	      let isDir = false;
   190	      try { isDir = fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory(); } catch { continue; }
   191	      if (!isDir) continue;
   192	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, dir)) { notes.push(`${b}: skipped (escapes archive/ — symlink?)`); continue; }
   193	      const fp = path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md');
   194	      if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) continue;
   195	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, fp)) { notes.push(`${b}: flow-state escapes archive/ (symlink?) — not read`); continue; }
   196	      const st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8'));
   197	      const step = st['current-step'];
   198	      if (step && step !== 'DONE' && step !== 'ABANDONED') notes.push(`archived ${b} @ ${step} — gate ④ possibly pending`);
   199	    }
   200	  }
   201	  if (!d7bad) checks.push(actives.length || notes.length
   202	    ? { id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') || `${actives.length} active change(s), flow-states valid` }
   203	    : { id: 'D7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no changes yet' });
   204	  else if (notes.length) checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') });
   205	
   206	  // D8 — legacy 3.x layout roots (existence-level, never followed or read through)
   207	  const legacy = legacyRoots(cwd);
   208	  checks.push(legacy.length
   209	    ? { id: 'D8', status: 'finding', detail: `legacy 3.x layout root(s) present: ${legacy.join(', ')}`, fix: 'migrate the artifacts into their change bundles — see MIGRATING.md (4.0): https://github.com/Apriorhythm/apriori-spec-development/blob/main/MIGRATING.md' }
   210	    : { id: 'D8', status: 'ok', detail: 'no legacy 3.x layout roots' });
   211	
   212	  return finish();
   213	}
   214	
   215	function toJson(res) {
   216	  return { result: res.result, findings: res.findings, checks: res.checks, errors: res.errors };
   217	}
   218	
   219	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori doctor [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--no-run] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]';
   220	
   221	function cli(argv) {
   222	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'doctor', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   223	    flags: { '--test-cmd': 'value', '--no-run': 'flag', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' },
   224	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ result: 'UNUSABLE', findings: 0, checks: [], errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   225	    const a = { testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null, noRun: !!f['--no-run'], cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'] };
   226	    const res = runDoctor(a);
   227	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   228	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('doctor: ' + e);
   229	    const mark = { ok: '✓', finding: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
   230	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.detail}${c.fix ? `  → ${c.fix}` : ''}`);
   231	    console.log(res.code === 2 ? '\nDOCTOR: UNUSABLE (see errors)'
   232	      : res.findings ? `\nDOCTOR: ${res.findings} finding(s)` : '\nDOCTOR: HEALTHY');
   233	    return res.code;
   234	  });
   235	}

   300	    if (!fs.existsSync(specsPath)) {
   301	      console.error(`error: spec store path does not exist: ${specsDir}` +
   302	        (fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'apriori')) ? '' : ` — is this an apriori project? (run 'apriori init')`));
   303	      console.log('\nRESULT: ERROR');
   304	      return 2;
   305	    }
   306	    const specFiles = [];
   307	    (function walk(d) {
   308	      for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   309	        const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   310	        if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p); else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) specFiles.push(p);
   311	      }
   312	    })(specsPath);

   314	    // batches through the shared matcher — a broken or pathological row is RESULT: ERROR (CK-15/16)
   315	    const resolved = resolveIdPattern(root, null);
   316	    if (resolved.error) {
   317	      console.error('error: ' + resolved.error);
   318	      console.log('\nRESULT: ERROR');
   319	      return 2;
   320	    }
   321	    const pairs = [];
   322	    for (const f of specFiles) {
   323	      const rel = path.relative(root, f);
   324	      for (const t of scenarioTitles(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'))) pairs.push([rel, t]);
   325	    }
   326	    const batch = makeIdMatcher(resolved).batch(pairs.map(([, t]) => t));
   327	    if (batch.failure) {
   328	      console.error(`error: process-config id-pattern matching failed (${batch.failure})`);
   329	      console.log('\nRESULT: ERROR');
   330	      return 2;
   331	    }
   332	    pairs.forEach(([rel, t], i) => {
   333	      if (batch.ids[i] === null) fails.push(`${rel}: scenario without a bindable ID: ${t.slice(0, 50)}`);
   334	    });
   335	
   336	    // CK-10: review-evidence secret tripwire (consumer mode; absent dir skips)
   337	    const secrets = checkReviewSecrets(root);
   338	    fails.push(...secrets.fails);
   339	    for (const w of secrets.warns) console.log('! ' + w);
   340	
   341	    if (self) {
   342	      // Handbook checks (apriori repo dogfooding) — run when the pairs exist.
   343	      // CK-08: a ONE-SIDED pair fails (a missing mirror must not skip silently);
   344	      // both sides absent → the pair is skipped (older checkouts unaffected).
   345	      const PAIRS = [['README.md', 'README_cn.md'], ['RUNBOOK.md', 'RUNBOOK_cn.md'], ['VISION.md', 'VISION_cn.md'],
   346	        ['docs/concepts.md', 'docs/concepts_cn.md'], ['docs/legacy.md', 'docs/legacy_cn.md'],
   347	        ['docs/ci.md', 'docs/ci_cn.md'], ['docs/cli.md', 'docs/cli_cn.md'],
   348	        ['docs/troubleshooting.md', 'docs/troubleshooting_cn.md']];
   349	      const files = {};
   350	      for (const [en, cn] of PAIRS) {
   351	        const pe = fs.existsSync(path.join(root, en)), pc = fs.existsSync(path.join(root, cn));
   352	        if (pe !== pc) fails.push(`one-sided EN/CN pair: ${pe ? cn : en} is missing`);
   353	        for (const nm of [en, cn]) {
   354	          const p = path.join(root, nm);
   355	          if (fs.existsSync(p)) files[nm] = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8');
   356	        }
   357	      }
   358	      for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(files)) {
   359	        fails.push(...checkLinks(path.dirname(path.join(root, name)), name, text));   // CK-09: per-file base
   360	        fails.push(...checkNoOpenspec(name, text));
   361	      }
   362	      for (const [en, cn] of PAIRS) if (files[en] && files[cn]) fails.push(...checkHeadingAlignment(en, files[en], cn, files[cn]));
   363	      if (Object.keys(files).length) fails.push(...checkVerdictPhrases(files));
   364	      if (files['RUNBOOK.md'] && files['RUNBOOK_cn.md']) fails.push(...checkCodexCommands(files['RUNBOOK.md'], files['RUNBOOK_cn.md']));
   365	      if (Object.keys(files).length) fails.push(...checkCodexKnownForms(files));
   366	      fails.push(...checkRunbookVersion(root, files));   // CK-11
   367	    }
   368	
   369	    for (const f of fails) console.log('✗ ' + f);
   370	    for (const w of checkRunbookFreshness(root)) console.log('! ' + w);   // CK-06: warn-only
   371	    console.log('\nRESULT:', fails.length ? `FAIL (${fails.length})` : 'PASS');
   372	    return fails.length ? 1 : 0;
   373	  });
   374	}

   343	function formatReport(v, results, fileCount, extras = {}) {
   344	  const lines = [];
   345	  const specCount = v.boundGreen.length + v.boundRed.length + v.unbound.length;
   346	  const tagged = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0);
   347	  const skipped = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + (r.skip || 0), 0);
   348	  lines.push(`specs: ${fileCount} file(s), ${specCount} identified scenario(s)`);
   349	  lines.push(`tests: ${tagged} tagged result(s)${skipped ? `, ${skipped} skipped (not counted)` : ''}\n`);
   350	  const groups = [['BOUND-GREEN', v.boundGreen], ['BOUND-RED', v.boundRed],
   351	                 ['UNBOUND (scenario, no test)', v.unbound], ['ORPHAN (test, no scenario)', v.orphan]];
   352	  for (const [label, items] of groups) {
   353	    if (!items.length) continue;
   354	    lines.push(`${label === 'BOUND-GREEN' ? '✓' : '✗'} ${label}: ${items.length}`);
   355	    for (const id of items) {
   356	      const r = results.get(id);
   357	      lines.push(`    ${id}${r ? `  ${r.pass}p/${r.fail}f${r.skip ? `/${r.skip}s` : ''}` : ''}`);
   358	    }
   359	  }
   360	  if (v.unattributed && v.unattributed.length) {
   361	    lines.push(`✗ UNATTRIBUTED FAILURES (not ok without a scenario ID): ${v.unattributed.length}`);
   362	    for (const raw of v.unattributed.slice(0, 20))
   363	      lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   364	    if (v.unattributed.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${v.unattributed.length - 20} more`);
   365	  }
   366	  if (v.unidentified.length) {
   367	    lines.push(`✗ UNIDENTIFIED (scenario, no ID — unbindable): ${v.unidentified.length}`);
   368	    for (const [f, t] of v.unidentified) lines.push(`    ${path.basename(f)}: ${t.slice(0, 60)}`);
   369	  }
   370	  if (extras.duplicates && extras.duplicates.length) {
   371	    lines.push(`✗ DUPLICATE scenario IDs (ambiguous binding): ${extras.duplicates.length}`);
   372	    for (const d of extras.duplicates) lines.push(`    ${d.id}: ${d.files.map((f) => path.basename(f)).join(', ')}`);
   373	  }
   374	  if (extras.stderr && extras.stderr.trim()) {
   375	    const sl = extras.stderr.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n').filter((l) => l.length);
   376	    lines.push(`— STDERR DIAGNOSTICS (not TAP): ${sl.length} line(s)`);
   377	    for (const raw of sl.slice(0, 20)) lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   378	    if (sl.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${sl.length - 20} more`);
   379	  }
   380	  const errs = extras.errors || [];
   381	  lines.push('\nRESULT: ' + (errs.length ? 'ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)'
   382	    : (v.clean && !(extras.duplicates || []).length ? 'GREEN — spec is the test suite' : 'GAPS')));
   383	  return lines.join('\n');
   384	}
   385	
   386	// Projection inputs for `verify --change` (all failure classes → run.errors, exit 2).
   387	// Roots resolve against cwd exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
   388	function buildChangeProjection(change, cwd) {
   389	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   390	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   391	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   392	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   393	  if (d.errors.length) return { projection, errors: d.errors, texts: null };
   394	  const p = am.buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, change);
   395	  projection.modules = p.modules;
   396	  projection.conflicts = p.conflicts;
   397	  projection.unstampedMutations = p.unstampedMutations;      // WARN grade — cli prints, verdict untouched
   398	  projection.notes = p.notes;
   399	  const errors = [...p.validation, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches];
   400	  if (p.conflicts.length) errors.push(`projection has ${p.conflicts.length} merge conflict(s) — no trustworthy projection: ${p.conflicts.join('; ')}`);
   401	  return { projection, errors, texts: errors.length ? null : p.texts };
   402	}
   403	
   404	// an errors-only run shape shared by every pre-test early exit (failed projection,
   405	// invalid pattern, matcher-channel failure) — fail closed WITHOUT running any tests
   406	function errorsRun(errors, projection) {
   407	  const verdict = evaluate(new Map(), [], new Map(), []);
   408	  const run = { verdict, results: new Map(), fileCount: 0, failCount: 0, scenarioCount: 0,
   409	    missingTargets: [], duplicates: [], bailout: null, noTap: false, tapProblems: [], stderr: '',
   410	    exec: { status: null, signal: null, error: null }, errors };
   411	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   412	  return run;
   413	}
   414	
   415	// enumeration-only projection metadata for the invalid-pattern early exit on --change runs:
   416	// SR-23's projection contract survives, yet no delta CONTENT is read (directory listing only)
   417	function enumerateProjection(change, cwd) {
   418	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   419	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   420	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   421	  if (!d.errors.length) projection.modules = d.files.map((f) => f.suffix);
   422	  return projection;
   423	}
   424	
   425	// Full verify run. opts: { specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd }
   426	function verify(opts) {
   427	  const cwd = opts.cwd || '.';
   428	  // pattern resolution FIRST — before any spec content is read or any test command spawned
   429	  const resolved = resolveIdPattern(cwd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   430	  if (resolved.error)
   431	    return errorsRun([resolved.error], opts.change ? enumerateProjection(opts.change, cwd) : null);
   432	  const idRe = new RegExp(resolved.source);
   433	  const matcher = makeIdMatcher(resolved);
   434	  let collected, projection = null;
   435	  if (opts.change) {
   436	    const b = buildChangeProjection(opts.change, cwd);
   437	    projection = b.projection;
   438	    if (!b.texts) return errorsRun(b.errors, projection);
   439	    collected = collectScenariosFromTexts(b.texts, idRe, matcher);
   440	  } else {
   441	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   442	  }
   443	  if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   444	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   445	  const exec = runTestCommand(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   446	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   447	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   448	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   449	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   450	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   451	    return run;
   452	  }
   453	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   454	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   455	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   456	  const run = {
   457	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   458	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   459	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   460	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   461	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   462	    tapProblems,
   463	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   464	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   465	  };
   466	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   467	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   468	  return run;
   469	}
   470	
   471	// machine-consumable shape for a verify run
   472	function verifyJson(run) {
   473	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   474	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 && run.errors.length === 0;
   475	  const json = projectionless(run, clean);
   476	  if (run.projection) json.projection = run.projection;   // ONLY --change runs carry this field
   477	  return json;
   478	}
   479	
   480	function projectionless(run, clean) {
   481	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   482	  return {
   483	    clean,
   484	    result: run.errors.length ? 'ERROR' : (clean ? 'GREEN' : 'GAPS'),
   485	    errors: run.errors,
   486	    specFiles: run.fileCount,
   487	    exec: run.exec,
   488	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   489	    boundGreen: v.boundGreen.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   490	    boundRed: v.boundRed.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   491	    unbound: v.unbound,
   492	    orphan: v.orphan.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   493	    unidentified: v.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })),
   494	    unattributedFailures: { count: (v.unattributed || []).length, lines: v.unattributed || [] },
   495	    stderr: run.stderr || '',
   496	  };
   497	}
   498	
   499	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   500	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   501	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   502	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   503	  return value;
   504	}
   505	
   506	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row;\n --id-pattern may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has an id-pattern row)';
   507	
   508	function cli(argv) {
   509	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   510	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   511	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   512	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   513	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   514	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   515	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   516	      return 2;
   517	    }
   518	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   519	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   520	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   521	      a.testCmd = c;
   522	    }
   523	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   524	    const run = verify(a);
   525	    if (run.projection)
   526	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   527	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   528	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   529	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   530	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr }));
   531	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   532	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   533	  });
   534	}
   535	
   536	module.exports = { leadId, stripFences, collectScenarios, collectScenariosFromTexts, parseTap, looksLikeTap,
   537	  zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, evaluate, infraErrors, formatReport, verifyJson, verify,
   538	  configTestCmd, cli, DEFAULT_ID, makeIdMatcher, _setChildRunner };

docs/cli.md:129:## 8. Configuration Reference
docs/cli.md:131:### 8.0 process-config keys: id-pattern
docs/cli.md:133:`| id-pattern | <bare JS regex source> |` in `apriori/process-config.md` declares the project's scenario-ID shape once, for every consumer. Resolution order: the `--id-pattern` flag (verify and gate only; judged by presence — an empty flag is an error, never a fallback) > the config row > the built-in default `[A-Z]+-\d+`. `check` (CK-04) and `doctor` (D6) consume the row with no flag. All four consumers recognize IDs through the same contract: the match starts at the title's first character, a following letter/digit/underscore rejects it, no `\b` is appended, the source compiles as written.
docs/cli.md:135:Pipe escaping has two layers — never conflate them: inside a table cell every pipe belonging to the value is written `\|` (so an alternation cell `(AC\|BR)-\d+` parses to the regex source `(AC|BR)-\d+`, where the bare `|` is alternation); a regex that must MATCH a literal pipe character uses a character class, written `[\|]` in the cell and parsing to `[|]`. This escape rule applies to every config key uniformly.
docs/cli.md:137:Errors are consumption-time and fail closed, naming their origin (`--id-pattern` or `process-config`): verify and gate exit 2 through their existing text/JSON error shapes, check prints `RESULT: ERROR` (exit 2), doctor reports a D6 finding and skips the D5 probe (result FINDINGS, exit 1) — never a silent fallback to the default. A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically, so its matching runs inside a terminable child process (killed on budget — a catastrophic-backtracking row cannot hang CI); the flag is operator-interactive input and runs in-process.
docs/cli.md:154:    - Give every scenario a stable ID (e.g. KV-03); downstream tests must reference these IDs (`apriori verify` binds them, `apriori check` rejects an ID-less scenario)
docs/cli.md:168:    - Stop when all done, `apriori verify` is GREEN, and the change is ready for archive (STEP6)
docs/cli.md:169:    - For any continue / silent-ignore / skip branch in the code, re-check the spec to confirm whether that branch must be user-visible; if the spec requires it, produce the corresponding record — don't satisfy only the "exclude the main path" while dropping the "display side"
docs/cli.md:170:    - Name every test after the scenario ID it covers (e.g. `test('KV-03 …')`); a spec scenario with no matching test fails `apriori verify`
